### [1] [AdaPilot: Towards Scene-Adaptive Policy Learning for Cross-Generator Text-to-Image Quality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29517)
*Wenjin Liu,Fayuan Ke,Yue Lu,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaPilot 将多轮图像生成建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过端到端强化学习学到一个场景自适应、可跨生成器迁移的通用质量优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升文生图质量的方法依赖特定生成器与特定任务，学到的能力难以泛化为通用的质量优化策略。

Method: 把多轮图像生成形式化为 MDP 并用端到端强化学习优化；将策略与生成器内部解耦以支持跨生成器迁移；引入场景感知奖励，使质量评估维度自适应匹配任务语义；采用过程级奖励建模图像质量的演化轨迹。

Result: 在生成质量与泛化性上优于基线；跨生成器评估显示单一策略可零样本迁移到未见过的生成器，并在所有评估生成器上保持正向平均增益。

Conclusion: AdaPilot 通过学习通用、可迁移的多轮质量优化策略，为文生图质量提升提供了场景自适应且跨生成器的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing methods for improving text-to-image generation quality have progressed from generator fine-tuning and prompt optimization to reinforcement learning with multi-turn visual feedback. However, existing strategies are deeply coupled with specific generators and tasks, and the learned capabilities are difficult to generalize into a universal quality optimization policy. Therefore, we propose AdaPilot, which learns a scene-adaptive, cross-generator transferable quality optimization policy by formulating multi-turn image generation as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and optimizing it via end-to-end reinforcement learning. Specifically, AdaPilot decouples the policy from generator internals to enable cross-generator transfer, introduces scene-aware rewards that adaptively align quality assessment dimensions with task semantics, and employs process-level rewards to model the evolution trajectory of image quality. Experimental results show AdaPilot outperforms baselines in generation quality and generalization. Separate cross-generator evaluations further show that a single policy transfers zero-shot to unseen generators while maintaining positive average gains across all evaluated generators. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQing/Ada_pilot.

</details>


### [2] [Albireo: Adaptive, Energy-Efficient Inference Framework for Video Object Detection on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29648)
*Amir Taherin,José Cano,Bin Ren,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Albireo：一种与检测器无关、无需编解码的自适应推理框架，用卡尔曼滤波按内容决定是否跳过检测器调用，在边缘设备上同时改善精度、能耗与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的视频目标检测需在长帧流上连续运行高开销检测器，导致能耗高、GPU 占用持续偏高。尽管相邻帧高度冗余，朴素的跳帧策略是内容盲的，会在目标进入、遮挡恢复和剧烈运动等关键时刻跳帧，从而损害检测质量。

Method: Albireo 无需修改或重训练检测器，可直接封装现成检测器。它为每个活动目标维护 10 维卡尔曼滤波（KF），仅当预测不确定性超过阈值时才调用检测器；在被跳过的帧上，用 KF 状态以近乎零 GPU 成本预测框。同时引入基于 KF 的救援机制，在检测器短暂漏检时保持已确认目标，避免输出碎片化；并用轻量空场景筛选避免在无目标帧调用检测器。该方法codec-free、content-aware。

Result: 在 BDD100K MOT 验证集上，使用三种架构不同的检测器（YOLO11x、YOLO26x、RF-DETR-Large）与两种 NVIDIA Jetson 平台（AGX Thor、AGX Orin）评测。所有配置下，Albireo 的 AP@50 相对逐帧推理保持在 ±1.2 pp 内，同时总能耗降低 12.1–17.6%。在 YOLO26x 上，AP@50 提升 +0.8 pp，能耗降低 17.6%（Thor）与 14.4%（Orin），单帧能量-延迟积分别降低 24.9% 与 26.1%。相比之下，固定间隔跳帧基线 FixedSkip-2（50% 跳帧率）损失 8.6 pp AP@50。代码、评测流程与逐片段结果已开源。

Conclusion: 基于内容与目标时序状态的自适应检测器调用调度，可在不牺牲甚至提升检测精度的前提下显著降低边缘端视频目标检测的能耗与延迟，显著优于固定跳帧策略，且具备检测器无关的通用性。

Abstract: Video object detection on edge devices runs computationally expensive detectors over long frame streams, causing high energy consumption and sustained GPU utilization. Although consecutive frames are highly redundant, naive frame skipping is content-blind: it skips during critical moments such as object entry, occlusion recovery, and abrupt motion, degrading detection quality. We present Albireo, a detector-agnostic, codec-free adaptive inference framework that wraps off-the-shelf detectors and decides when detector invocation can be safely skipped based on scene content and per-object temporal state, requiring no detector modification or retraining. Albireo maintains a 10-dimensional Kalman filter (KF) per active object and invokes the detector only when prediction uncertainty exceeds a threshold; on skipped frames, boxes are predicted from the KF state at near-zero GPU cost. A KF-based rescue mechanism preserves confirmed objects through brief detector misses to prevent output fragmentation, while a lightweight empty-scene screen avoids detector calls on objectless frames. We evaluate Albireo on the BDD100K MOT validation split with three architecturally distinct detectors (YOLO11x, YOLO26x, RF-DETR-Large) on two NVIDIA Jetson platforms (AGX Thor, AGX Orin). Across all configurations, Albireo keeps AP@50 within +/-1.2 pp of per-frame inference while reducing total energy by 12.1-17.6%. On YOLO26x, it improves AP@50 by +0.8 pp while reducing energy by 17.6% (Thor) and 14.4% (Orin) and per-frame energy-delay product by 24.9% and 26.1%, respectively. Thus, the default operating point improves accuracy, energy, and latency together. In contrast, FixedSkip-2, a fixed-interval baseline with a 50% skip rate, loses 8.6 pp AP@50. Source code, evaluation pipeline, and per-clip results are available at https://github.com/amirtaherin/albireo

</details>

### [3] [ModularSQL: A Runtime Guardrail for the Multiplicity Blind Spot in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29573)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示Text-to-SQL评估中的"多重性盲区"（MBS），提出保留多重性的评估指标Multiset-EX与轻量级运行时防护栏ModularSQL，以低开销缩小基准准确率与执行安全SQL之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统在生产数据库中部署时，即使通过基准评估的查询仍可能扭曲下游工作流。标准集合式执行准确率（Set-EX）会折叠重复行，从而遗漏缺失DISTINCT、聚合膨胀、笛卡尔式连接爆炸等多重性错误，导致基准准确率不能代表执行安全的SQL。

Method: 作者将该问题定义为"多重性盲区"（MBS），提出保留多重性的评估标准Multiset-EX；并进一步提出ModularSQL，一种轻量级后选择运行时防护栏，探测执行结果中的多重性异常，仅对标记查询应用确定性补丁或低成本LLM补救。

Result: 在可执行BIRD-Dev（N=1532）上，三个主干（Qwen2.5-Coder-32B、Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B、Gemma-3-27B）的DeepEye-SQL制品中Set-EX与Multiset-EX间存在5.81–6.79 pp的一致差距；该差距在DAIL-SQL+GPT-4（5.22 pp）与BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo（3.39 pp）上同样存在。集成Qwen3-Coder的ModularSQL在保持Set-EX 72.06%的同时将Multiset-EX从65.86%提升至67.75%（+1.89 pp），标记77个高风险异常，仅增加0.0076美元LLM成本与每查询120 ms摊销延迟；跨流水线评估显示候选无关检测器与确定性补丁也可迁移至独立发布的预测集。

Conclusion: 基准准确率并不必然意味着执行安全的SQL；轻量级、感知多重性的运行时防护栏能以适度的计算开销缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL systems are increasingly deployed on production databases, where queries that pass benchmark evaluation can still produce results that distort downstream workflows. Standard set-based execution accuracy (Set-EX) collapses duplicate rows and can therefore miss multiplicity errors, including missing DISTINCT, inflated aggregates, and Cartesian-style join explosions.
  We call this the Multiplicity Blind Spot (MBS) and introduce Multiset-EX, a multiplicity-preserving evaluation criterion that exposes such failures. Across released DeepEye-SQL artifacts from three backbones (Qwen2.5-Coder-32B, Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, and Gemma-3-27B) on executable BIRD-Dev N=1532, we find a consistent 5.81--6.79 pp gap between Set-EX and Multiset-EX. The gap is not specific to DeepEye-SQL: it persists on released DAIL-SQL+GPT-4 (5.22 pp) and BIRD GPT-3.5-turbo (3.39 pp) predictions.
  We further introduce ModularSQL, a lightweight post-selection runtime guardrail that probes executed results for multiplicity anomalies and applies deterministic patches or low-cost LLM rescue only to flagged queries. Integrated with DeepEye-SQL using Qwen3-Coder, ModularSQL preserves Set-EX at 72.06% while improving Multiset-EX from 65.86% to 67.75% (+1.89 pp). It flags 77 high-risk anomalies, while adding only $0.0076 in total LLM cost and 120 ms amortized latency per query. Cross-pipeline evaluation shows that the candidate-free detector and deterministic patches also transfer to independently released prediction sets. Overall, these results show that benchmark accuracy does not necessarily imply execution-safe SQL, and that lightweight, multiplicity-aware runtime guardrails can narrow this gap with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [4] [Stochastic Semantic Evidence Graphs: Uncertainty Propagation and Governance for Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29703)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出随机语义证据图（SSEG）框架，将AI智能体评估从"只看最终答案"转为对不确定性来源与传播路径的定量溯源与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体评估通常只检验最终答案，但错误可能来自证据、检索、提示、生成或决策映射等多个环节，缺乏对不确定性进入位置与传播方式的细粒度刻画与使用资格判定。

Method: 构建分层随机有向无环图：语言节点展开为自回归token子图，可观测输出为完整短语上的分布律；支持可选的语义归约与校准。定义图相对的局部缺陷与下游边影响，推导终端误差的路径界，并用其逐节点项触发治理诊断；在来源溯源上保留不确定的主张—段落关系，传播尖锐Fréchet界而非假设来源独立。

Result: 在三种开放权重架构上，信息等价改动会显著改变完整短语分布律；受控实验中5000个案例未出现证书违背；交叉RAG与实时Brave检索实验分离出检索、呈现、来源与交互效应。

Conclusion: SSEG把工作流溯源转化为对"不确定性从何进入、如何传播、输出是否有资格被使用"的定量说明，为智能体评估与治理提供可操作依据。

Abstract: AI-agent evaluations usually inspect a final answer, yet error may enter through evidence, retrieval, prompting, generation or decision mapping. We introduce a stochastic semantic evidence graph (SSEG), a hierarchical stochastic DAG whose language node expands into an autoregressive token subgraph and whose observable output may be a law over complete phrases. Semantic reduction and calibration are optional. We define graph-relative local defects and downstream edge influences, derive a pathwise bound on terminal error and use its nodewise terms to diagnose governance triggers. For source provenance, the graph preserves uncertain claim--passage relations and propagates sharp Fréchet bounds rather than assuming independence across sources. Across three open-weight architectures, information-equivalent changes materially alter complete-phrase laws. A controlled experiment yields no certificate violations in 5,000 cases; crossed-RAG and live Brave-retrieval experiments separate retrieval, presentation, source and interaction effects. SSEG therefore turns workflow provenance into a quantitative account of where uncertainty entered, how it propagated and whether an output is qualified for use.

</details>


### [5] [TimeBraid: Unifying Time Series and Language for Understanding and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29792)
*Xinyue Wang,Jiacheng Pang,Kun Zhou,Kexin Zhang,Defu Cao,Fan Feng,Faisal,Songyao Jin,Yan Liu,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TimeBraid 通过交错全局残差注意力层对齐预训练语言模型与预训练时间序列基础模型，构建统一的时序-语言模型系列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合语言模型与时序基础模型之间的模态鸿沟，使模型同时具备语言的指令跟随与推理能力和时序的连续信号感知与零样本预测能力。

Method: 通过交错全局残差注意力层对齐两个预训练表示空间，探索对齐位置、时序结构接地、理解与生成平衡、联合优化稳定性等设计选择；采用统一提示方案、稳定联合训练，并使用220万条时序-文本配对与490万条指令微调样本监督。

Result: 在时序感知、理解、推理以及上下文辅助与单模态预测等基准上，TimeBraid 与规模远大于自身的通用模型及任务专用模型相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 统一时序与语言的建模是可行的，共享表示空间下可实现跨模态理解与生成，为构建融合连续信号感知与语言推理的统一基础模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We present TimeBraid, a series of unified time-series and language models that align pretrained language models and pretrained time-series foundation models through interleaved global residual attention layers. Each model inherits knowledge, instruction following, and reasoning from one side, continuous-signal perception and zero-shot forecasting from the other, and fuses the two in a shared representation space where both modalities are understood and generated. We study the design choices that make such unified modeling work: where to align the two representation spaces, how to ground language in temporal structure, how to balance understanding with generation, and how to keep joint optimization stable. The resulting recipe combines a unified prompting scheme for diverse time-series and text tasks, stabilized joint training, and supervision from 2.2M curated series--text pairs and 4.9M instruction-tuning samples. Across benchmarks spanning time-series perception, understanding, reasoning, and both context-aided and unimodal forecasting, TimeBraid remains competitive with far larger general-purpose models and task-specific counterparts.

</details>


### [6] [An Empirical Study of VLM Pipelines for Long-Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29933)
*Kenan E. Ak,Jay Mohta,Gwang Gook Lee,Yan Xu,Dimitrios Dimitriadis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了长文档视觉语言模型（VLM）应用中的三类关键设计选择：文档输入方式、检索器选择以及静态流水线与智能体（agentic）流水线的取舍，并给出多组基准实验结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 越来越多用于长文档处理（文本与图表、表格、复杂版式混合），但实际部署时面临诸多未明确的工程决策：如何把文档喂给模型、只送部分页面时该用哪种检索器、以及是否应采用智能体式流程还是静态流水线。论文的动机是对这些选择进行系统性的实证研究。

Method: 在两个长文档问答基准（MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL）上开展实验，同时使用前沿 API 模型与开源权重 VLM。构建了一个包含页面、表格、图形和搜索调用共六种工具的智能体，并与静态页面输入流水线对比；比较图像检索与文本检索两种模态、以及轻量 cross-encoder 重排与重型多阶段 LLM 流水线；分析是否按问题证据类型进行路由，并与“按题选最优流水线”的 oracle 上界对比。

Result: （1）六工具智能体的收益依赖于阅读器规模：Qwen3.5-4B/9B 时不如静态页面输入，Qwen3.5-27B 时持平，Sonnet 4.5 时领先；在 LongDocURL 上各阅读器均持平或领先，但优势在 LongDocURL 上缩小至噪声范围。（2）检索模态比具体检索器更重要：最强图像检索器优于最强文本流水线；文本侧单个现成 cross-encoder 重排基本可媲美重型多阶段 LLM 流水线；Top-k 图像检索在每个阅读器下都是最省 token 的输入方式，约为全页输入的 1/7 至 1/4。（3）三种最强流水线在不同问题上各有成功，oracle 按题选优可提升约 13 个点，但按证据类型路由几乎无法回收这部分增益。

Conclusion: 长文档 VLM 系统的部署效果并非由单一最优方案决定；智能体流程的收益取决于回答模型的能力规模，检索模态（图像优于文本）比具体检索器更关键，且图像 Top-k 检索在 token 效率上明显占优。最强流水线之间具有互补性，但简单的证据类型路由难以释放这种互补增益，说明按题自适应选择流水线仍有较大开放空间。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used for long-document processing, where the inputs combine text with charts, tables, figures, and complex layouts. Deploying them means choosing how to feed the document to the model, which retriever to use when only a subset of pages is sent, and whether to run the model agentically or as a static pipeline. We study these choices on two long-document QA benchmarks with both frontier API and open-weight VLMs. First, on MMLongBench-Doc our six-tool agent with page, table, figure, and search calls pays off only once the answering VLM is large enough: with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B it trails static page input, with Qwen3.5-27B it draws level, and with Sonnet 4.5 it leads. On LongDocURL it is level with or ahead of static input at every reader. Its lead over the strongest static pipeline is clearest with the frontier reader on MMLongBench-Doc and narrows to within noise on LongDocURL. Second, retrieval modality matters more than the specific retriever: the strongest image retriever leads the strongest text pipeline, and on the text side a single off-the-shelf cross-encoder rerank essentially matches a much heavier multi-stage LLM pipeline. Top-k image retrieval is also the most token-efficient input at every reader we paired it with, at roughly a seventh to a quarter of the tokens of sending every page. Third, cutting across all three choices, three of our strongest pipelines succeed on different questions, and an oracle that picks the best pipeline per question gains roughly thirteen points over the best single pipeline, though evidence-type routing recovers almost none of it.

</details>


### [7] [Return or Revise? Learning When Revision Helps Retrieval-Augmented QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30087)
*Nicholas Kashani Motlagh,Tim Anderson,Jeremy Gwinnup,Grant Erdmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"可恢复性"(recoverability)概念,用配对结果训练策略预测修订效果,决定是返回草稿还是用检索证据修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 答案修订系统需要决定是否用检索证据修订已有草稿答案,但仅靠草稿置信度只能判断当前答案是否正确,无法估计指定修订带来的效果。

Method: 在离线训练与评估中,用同一正确性评判器对草稿和候选修订同时打分,使修复、危害及与oracle的差距可观测,称之为配对效应(recoverability);训练策略在修订前预测该效应。

Result: 在25,870个开放域问题、三种修订设置下,基于配对结果的评分器在全部九个Llama设置-种子拟合中AUC均优于草稿正确性评分器,平均提升0.23–0.68个准确率点(仅密集检索显著);策略优于总是修订,缩小三分之一以上oracle差距,但仍执行38–46%有害修订;若有草稿无关的标准RAG答案,二选一更强(约2–4点),再加修订作为第三选项无显著增益。

Conclusion: 可恢复性刻画的是单次修订,其作为可选动作的价值还取决于其他备选项。

Abstract: We consider the decision of whether to return an existing draft answer or revise it using retrieved evidence, as in answer-revision systems. Draft confidence estimates whether the current answer is correct, but the decision requires estimating the effect of a specified revision. For offline training and evaluation, we grade both the returned draft and its candidate revision under the same correctness judge, which makes repair, harm, and the gap to an oracle observable. We call this paired effect its recoverability, and we train policies to predict it before revision. On 25,870 held-out open-domain questions across three revision setups, a scorer trained on the paired outcome has greater area under the accuracy--revision-rate curve than a matched draft-correctness scorer in all nine Llama setup--seed fits, and gains 0.23--0.68 accuracy points on average at development-selected thresholds, a difference significant across training runs only for dense retrieval. The resulting policy improves on always revising and on average closes more than a third of the oracle gap, although it still applies 38--46% of the harmful revisions. When a draft-free standard-RAG answer is also available, however, choosing between the draft and that answer is stronger by about two points for Llama and four for OLMo, and adding candidate revision as a third option yields no significant gain. Recoverability describes one revision; its value as an available action also depends on the alternatives.

</details>


### [8] [R-DEIM Net: An Efficient Rationale-Augmented Dual-Expert Interaction Model for Paraphrase Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30100)
*Pushp,Vaibhav Prajapati,Himangshu Sarma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出76M参数的R-DEIM Net双专家架构，在Quora Question Pairs上达到90.07%准确率，兼顾释义检测准确性与可读依据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有释义检测存在权衡：大语言模型准确率高但计算开销大，而高效的Siamese-BERT变体虽可扩展但缺乏可解释的依据生成。本文探索中等规模模型能否在保持竞争力准确率的同时生成人类可读的依据。

Method: R-DEIM Net包含两个专家组件：交互专家通过多尺度2D卷积和注意力头捕捉token级相似模式，支持可变输入长度；推理专家使用Flan-T5-small解码器生成依据作为辅助监督。模型不重新编码生成文本，而是提取并池化解码器隐藏状态作为分类的补充特征。

Result: 在Quora Question Pairs数据集上，通过10折交叉验证取得90.07%准确率和90.16% F1值，与强Transformer基线（如MFAE BERT的90.54%）及LLaMA-70B等大模型方法相比具有竞争力，且参数量显著更小。

Conclusion: 中等规模双专家架构可在释义检测中实现与大模型竞争的准确率，并在预测的同时生成依据，为提供辅助性人类可读描述提供了潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in paraphrase detection reveal a fundamental trade-off: large language models achieve high accuracy but require high computation, while efficient Siamese-BERT variants offer practical scalability with reduced transparency in rationale generation. We present R-DEIM Net, a 76M-parameter dual-expert architecture exploring whether moderate-scale models can achieve competitive accuracy on paraphrase detection while enabling human-readable rationale generation. The architecture combines two specialized components: an Interaction Expert that captures token-level similarity patterns through multi-scale 2D convolutions and attention head allowing variable input length, and a Reasoning Expert that uses a Flan-T5-small decoder to generate rationales as auxiliary supervision. Rather than re-encoding generated text, we extract and pool decoder hidden states as complementary features for classification. On the Quora Question Pairs dataset, R-DEIM Net achieves 90.07\% accuracy and 90.16\% F1-score via 10-fold cross-validation. This represents competitive performance with strong transformer-based baselines (e.g., MFAE BERT: 90.54\% accuracy) and recent large language model based approaches (LLaMA-70B) while using a substantially smaller parameter budget. The model generates rationales alongside predictions, providing potential for auxiliary human-readable descriptions.

</details>


### [9] [Does a model's stated reason for rejecting a candidate do any work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30151)
*Archit Rastogi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需评判模型即可检验语言模型解释性陈述的方法：将模型声称对手缺失的事实句子插入其资料中，再次询问其选择，并用长度匹配的无关句及未被提及的第三选项作为对照，发现"被指名对手处补入事实"能显著改变模型选择，但最强效应来自无关句的位置效应，故结论应视为对效应范围的界定而非确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在候选选择任务中常以"某候选缺少某事实"为由拒绝对手，这类陈述是关于输入文本的可证伪主张，但既有评估多依赖主观评判，缺乏无需评判者的客观检验手段，作者希望建立一种直接可测的因果性检验设计。

Method: 在2WikiMultihopQA上对六个开源模型做贪心解码的选择实验。将模型指名的缺失事实句真实插入被指名对手的资料中再询问；设置两个对照：长度匹配的无关句插在同一资料处，以及同样两句插入模型从未提及的第三选项处。统计采用比值比与Holm校正，并进行交叉验证、共候选提及/关系模板/流畅度的事后匹配、强制单token概率读取，以及字符串规则测量的人工验证。

Result: 最大规模运行中，在被指名对手处补入事实相比无关对照能改变选择，OR=3.57 [1.54, 8.26]，Holm p=0.0210，且剔除任一单模型后仍成立；设计原本要检测的对比（同样事实放在无人指名的选项处）未通过校正（Holm p=0.2428）。最强效应不含内容主张：同一无关句在被指名对手处比在第三选项处更能改变选择（Holm p=0.0008）。事后匹配共候选提及与关系模板可保持内容效应方向，匹配流畅度则削弱其一；强制单token概率读取在该对比上与自由文本选择方向相反，三种解释均无支持。验证测量规则时发现八处缺陷，其中最大的选择解析规则错误率17.1%，若不修正会把四个存活对比误报为六个。

Conclusion: 模型解释中"对手缺失某事实"的说法具有可测量的因果效应，但该效应同时受位置等非内容因素影响，且测量规则本身易出错，因此内容对比只能用于界定效应边界，不能据此确立内容因果性；模型选择与概率读出一致性存疑，需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Asked to choose between candidates and explain the choice, a language model often rejects a rival by naming a fact its profile lacks: no director, no date of death. That sentence is a claim about the text in front of the model, and it can be tested without any judge. We insert a real corpus sentence stating the named fact into the rival's profile and ask again under greedy decoding. Two controls separate content from placement: a length-matched irrelevant sentence at the same profile, and the same two sentences at a third option the model never mentioned. In the largest of three runs, six open models on 2WikiMultihopQA, supplying the named fact at the profile the model named moves its choice more than the irrelevant control does, odds ratio 3.57 [1.54, 8.26], Holm p=0.0210, and this survives dropping any single model. The contrast the design was built to detect, the same fact at the option nobody named, does not clear correction, Holm p=0.2428. The strongest result in the family carries no content claim at all: the identical irrelevant sentence moves the choice more at the named rival than at the third option, Holm p=0.0008. Repair and control also differ in co-candidate mentions, relation template and fluency; post-hoc matching on the first two preserves the content effects' direction, matching fluency weakens one, so the content contrasts bound an effect rather than establish one. A forced single-token probability read disagrees in direction with the free-text choice on that same contrast, and three candidate explanations for the disagreement find no support. Every measurement is a string rule, so each was validated against the records it reads; validation caught eight defects. The largest, a choice-parsing rule that returned the option a model had just rejected in 17.1% of adjudicable responses, would have reported six surviving contrasts instead of four.

</details>


### [10] [Do Audio Language Models Hear and Read Distinctive Features Alike?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30167)
*Yuanhao Chen,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探究音频语言模型的单一解码器在"听到"与"读到"同一音素时，是否沿同一方向表征同一区别性特征。作者用最小对立对的平均表征偏移构建每个流的特征方向并计算余弦相似度，以随机配对构建参考基线，在6个模型、7个特征、11个语系15种语言上测试。结果显示仅Qwen2.5-Omni两个模型的清浊特征超过多重检验校正后的基线，且基线随模型差异达7倍；6个模型中有3个在音频侧对清浊特征方向一致，模型家族（而非规模）决定哪个流表征某特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频语言模型将语音与文本送入同一解码器，但尚不清楚该共享解码器是否在"听觉"和"阅读"同一音素时以相同方向表征同一区别性特征。论文旨在检验语音流与文本流表征的几何一致性，并探讨模型规模与模型家族哪个更能预测特征的流归属。

Method: 针对仅差一个特征的最小音素对，计算两名成员平均表征之差，再对多组差异取平均，得到每个流的特征方向，并测量两流方向间的余弦相似度。由于两流对任意音素对已具有一致性，作者构建随机配对参考基线，而非以零为参照。实验覆盖6个模型、7个音素特征、来自11个语系的15种语言，并进行多重检验校正。

Result: 仅Qwen2.5-Omni的两个模型在多重检验校正后其清浊（voicing）特征超过随机参考基线；参考基线本身在模型间相差约7倍。6个模型中有3个在音频流上对清浊特征呈现跨14种语言（最小对立对数量足够）的统一方向，其中2个模型在所有语言对上均一致。

Conclusion: 共享解码器并不普遍在语音与文本流中以相同方向表征同一音素特征，多数模型和特征未超过基线。决定某特征由哪个流表征的是模型家族而非模型规模，提示架构与训练配方比参数量更关键。

Abstract: Audio language models pass speech and text through a single decoder. We ask whether that decoder represents a distinctive feature in the same direction when a phoneme is heard and when it is read. For minimal pairs of phonemes differing in one feature, we take the offset between the two members' mean representations. Averaging those offsets gives a direction for each stream, and we measure the cosine between the two. Because the two streams already agree about arbitrary phoneme pairs, we compare every measure against a reference built from random pairings rather than against zero. We apply this to 6 models, 7 features and 15 languages from 11 families. Only voicing in the two Qwen2.5-Omni models exceeds that reference after correction for multiple testing, and the reference varies by a factor of seven between models. In three of the six models, voicing has one direction in audio across the 14 languages with enough minimal pairs to measure it, and every language pair agrees in two of them. The model family, not the model size, predicts which stream represents a feature.

</details>


### [11] [Agentic Detection of Online Conspiracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30250)
*Lior Biton,Oren Tsur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将阴谋论言论检测重新定义为社会语境嵌入的意图推断任务，并提出一个配备社交查询工具的智能体框架，在希伯来语推特大规模数据集上验证其优于纯文本分类及非智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的阴谋论话语并不总是通过明确主张或稳定词汇标记表达；相同表面内容可能表达支持、合理关切、批评、讽刺或嘲弄。因此核心挑战不仅是识别阴谋论相关主张，还要推断说话者的意图（即话语的言外之力），这需要借助相关社会语境。

Method: 提出一个智能体框架（agentic framework），配备一组支持社交查询的工具，用于按需检索相关社会语境。使用一个独特的希伯来语推文数据集，覆盖2018年末至2023年初四年间80%--90%的公共希伯来语推文，涵盖多个选举周期及新冠疫情期间的疫苗接种活动，以恢复不同社会语境。在人工标注的对抗性数据集上评估，对比纯文本分类、非智能体但可访问相同语境的模型等设置，并分析结果、错误及效率（token经济性）权衡。

Result: 语境感知工作流始终优于纯文本分类；智能体框架显著优于其他框架和设置，包括可访问相同语境的非智能体模型。研究还提供了对结果、错误和效率（token经济性）权衡的分析。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持将阴谋论检测视为一种社会嵌入的解释任务，其中有效分类不仅取决于获得语境，还取决于自适应推理——智能体按个案使用工具，仅请求与当前推理步骤相关的证据。

Abstract: Conspiratorial discourse on social media is not always expressed through explicit claims or stable lexical markers. The same surface content may express endorsement, legitimate concerns, criticism, satire, or mockery. The main challenge is therefore not only recognizing conspiracy-related claims, but inferring the speaker's intent -- the utterance's illocutionary force. We argue that this can be achieved through the use of relevant social contexts and propose an agentic framework, equipped with a set of tools supporting social queries.
  We demonstrate the benefits of our approach on a unique dataset of Hebrew tweets, covering 80\%--90\% of the public Hebrew tweets published over a four-year span (late 2018-- early 2023), encompassing several election cycles as well as the COVID pandemic years and related vaccination campaigns. This extensive coverage can be used in recovering different social contexts. Evaluating our framework on a manually-annotated adversarial dataset, we find that context-aware workflows consistently outperform text-only classification and that the agentic framework performs significantly better than other frameworks and settings, including a non-agentic model exposed to the same contexts available to the agent. We further provide an analysis of the results, the errors and efficiency (token economy) tradeoffs.
  These findings support viewing the task of conspiracy detection as a socially embedded interpretation task, in which effective classification depends not only on access to contexts, but also on adaptive reasoning in which the agent uses tools on a per-case basis, asking only for evidence relevant to its current reasoning step.

</details>


### [12] [Rufus-Air: An Open LLM Post-Training Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29421)
*Chia-Yuan Chang,Renyuan Cheng,Rui Feng,Xiaotian Han,Yuan He,Hongye Jin,Linwei Li,Shiyang Li,Fenglin Liu,Xin Liu,Priyanka Nigam,Haoyang Wen,Zhenghao Xu,Zhuocheng Xu,Bing Yin,Qingyu Yin,Chao Zhang,Rongzhi Zhang,Zhihan Zhang,Zixuan Zhang,Zixuan Zhang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rufus-Air 是一个开放可复现的后训练配方，基于 GLM-4.5-Air-Base（106B-A12B），由八个阶段串行构成，性能优于官方发布版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机是提供一套开放、可复现的大模型后训练配方，不依赖新的人工标注或内部蒸馏教师模型，仅基于开源组件与公开数据，以揭示后训练各阶段的数据、奖励设计、基础设施与阶段顺序等关键要素。

Method: 方法是将后训练组织为八阶段串行流水线：SFT、推理RL、代码RL、指令遵循RL、通用智能体、代码智能体、搜索智能体、RLHF；阶段从基础能力推进到高级能力，从硬性可验证奖励过渡到较软的基于评判的奖励信号，并记录数据、奖励设计、基础设施与阶段化结果。

Result: 结果显示：多样化高质量SFT奠定坚实能力下限；难度过滤使RL提示保持在有效学习区间；奖励可靠性为阶段排序提供实用原则；基础设施与工程选择本身属于配方的一部分。Rufus-Air 相较官方 GLM-4.5-Air 后训练版本有所提升，并与同规模开源模型具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结论是后训练配方可通过开源组件与公开数据实现开放复现，且数据质量、难度过滤、奖励可靠性排序与工程基础设施是决定效果的核心要素。

Abstract: Rufus-Air is an open and reproducible post-training recipe on GLM-4.5-Air-Base (106B-A12B), organized as a serial pipeline of eight stages: SFT, Reasoning RL, Coding RL, Instruction-Following RL, General Agent, Coding Agent, Search Agent, and RLHF. We document the data, reward design, infrastructure, stage order, and stagewise results needed to reproduce the recipe. Stages progress from basic to advanced capabilities and from hard, verifiable rewards to softer judge-based signals. Training builds on open-source components and public data, much of it used as released, without new human annotation or an in-house distillation teacher. Our main findings are that (i) diverse, high-quality SFT establishes a strong capability floor; (ii) difficulty filtering keeps RL prompts within a productive learning range; (iii) reward reliability provides a practical principle for ordering stages; and (iv) infrastructure and engineering choices are part of the recipe, not just an implementation detail. Rufus-Air improves over the official GLM-4.5-Air post-trained release and is competitive with similarly sized open models.

</details>


### [13] [Operator Packages, Proposer Strength, and Construction-Family Plateaus in Office-Scale Verified Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29636)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对一个极简的 FunSearch 式“提议—验证—选择”搜索循环做了受控因子实验，发现“示意笔记本记忆 + 已发现构造的行为排斥”这一算子组合能显著缩小种子到记录的差距，且增益近似可加、无正交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已验证搜索（模型提议程序、硬评估器打分、选择保留最优）已在数学问题上刷新记录，但针对“提议方组件”的受控消融实验仍很稀少。作者希望在办公级、低成本条件下系统检验各提议算子的独立贡献与交互作用。

Method: 在单台笔记本上用 30B 本地模型搭建最小 FunSearch 式循环（每次运行 120–600 个已验证样本），设置三个提议算子：①模型自写并携带的示意笔记本（替代逐字精英保留）；②命名障碍；③对已发现构造的行为排斥。在公共仓库的 9 个构造问题上运行完整 2^3 析因设计并重复两次；另用前沿模型在相同循环下做对照与范围界定实验，并对停滞处做注册式族提示测试。

Result: 析因分析支持主对比：该组合多填补了种子到记录差距的 +0.196（合并 p=0.023，阶段组合 p≈0.08，逐问题中位效应 +0.045）。排斥在各处提升构造哈希多样性（p=0.0039，部分属操纵检验）。未发现记忆×排斥的正交互（界在约 ±0.04 内），增益可加性分解；记忆+排斥是唯一从未崩溃的组（18 次运行 0 次崩溃），与完整组差距在 0.025 内。前沿提议者在几十个样本内即达到本地模型数百样本未达到的水平，且在其单次范围运行中无需这些算子。旗舰问题上搜索在填补约 92% 差距后停滞：族提示以文字给出时被采纳却变差，以代码给出时被优化，但最佳有限网格实现仍低于无辅助达到的平台；无辅助运行未自行产生该思路。

Conclusion: 在办公级规模下，提议方算子（尤其示意记忆与行为排斥的组合）可稳定、可加地提升已验证搜索的收敛与多样性，且该组合最为鲁棒；但算子并非前沿模型增益的来源，搜索在接近记录时仍会停滞，且对“思路级”提示的搬运与优化能力有限。作者公开了实验框架、全部候选与带日期的预注册。

Abstract: Verified search, in which a language model proposes programs, a hard evaluator scores them, and selection keeps the best, has recently moved mathematical records; controlled ablations of the proposer-side components remain rare. We instrument a minimal FunSearch-style loop at office scale (a 30B local model on a laptop, 120-600 verified samples per run) with three operator packages: a schematic notebook the model writes and carries instead of verbatim elites, a named obstacle, and behavioural repulsion from constructions already found. On nine construction problems from a public repository, the complete 2^3 factorial with two replicates favours the primary contrast in a nominal two-stage analysis: the composition closes more of the seed-to-record gap (+0.196; nominal pooled p=0.023, stage-combination p~0.08; median per-problem effect +0.045). Repulsion raises construction-hash diversity everywhere (p=0.0039; partly a manipulation check). The factorial finds no positive memory-by-repulsion interaction (bounded to about +/-0.04); the gain decomposes additively, and memory+repulsion is the only arm that never collapses (0 of 18 runs), within 0.025 of the full composition. A frontier proposer under the identical loop reaches in tens of samples what the local model does not in hundreds; in single scoping runs its gains arrive without the operators. The search stalls after closing ~92% of the gap on the flagship problem, and the registered family-hint test gives the stall its first reading: named in words, the reference family is adopted and loses; handed as code, it is optimized, but our best finite-grid implementation remains below the plateau reached unaided. The loop transported and optimized the idea it was handed; no unaided run produced it. We release the harness, every candidate, and the dated pre-registrations.

</details>


### [14] [IterSynth: Rethinking Deep Search Agents via Role-Decoupled Iterative Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29444)
*Xingyu Wu,Yuchen Yan,Zhengxi Lu,Siqi Chen,Xin ZHANG,Aiting Liu,Chao Deng,Jie Liu,Jin Ma,Jian Shao,Jun Xiao,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 IterSynth：角色解耦与摘要式深度搜索范式，配合 RDPO 强化学习训练，在 5 个长程深度搜索基准上 8B 模型平均得分 50.7，超最强同类约 4.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 式 LLM agent 在深度搜索中存在两大局限：一是角色耦合，单一策略需同时负责规划、证据利用与答案综合；二是上下文累积，不断增长的搜索历史带来噪声，淹没有用信息。作者希望解耦能力、压缩上下文以提升长程搜索表现。

Method: 提出 IterSynth 范式：在 Planner（识别信息需求）与 Synthesizer（将证据整合进持续演化的摘要状态）之间交替，用摘要作为搜索的持久状态，从而分离规划与综合并减少上下文噪声。为有效训练，提出 Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization（RDPO）强化学习方法，结合最终结果奖励与轮级评分标准评估，并计算角色专属优势以实现更精确的信用分配。

Result: 在 BrowseComp、Xbench-DS 等五个长程深度搜索基准上，IterSynth-8B 平均得分 50.7，超过此前最强的 ≤8B agent 约 4.2%。同时该方法可作为模型无关的提示范式，在前沿闭源模型上相较 ReAct 及类似提示范式取得显著零样本提升。

Conclusion: 角色解耦与摘要状态化设计能有效缓解角色耦合与上下文累积问题，RDPO 提供更精细的信用分配；IterSynth 既可作为高效小模型的训练方案，也可作为通用提示范式迁移到更大模型，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Deep search requires LLM agents to decompose complex queries, search for evidence, and synthesize grounded answers, yet existing ReAct-style agents suffer from two limitations: role coupling, where one policy must handle planning, evidence use, and synthesis; and context accumulation, where growing search histories introduce noise and obscure useful information. To address these issues, we propose IterSynth, a role-decoupled and summary-based paradigm that alternates between a Planner for identifying information needs and a Synthesizer for integrating evidence into an evolving summary state. This design separates planning from synthesis while using the summary as the persistent state of search, reducing both capability coupling and context noise. To train IterSynth effectively, we further introduce Role-Decoupled Policy Optimization (RDPO) for reinforcement learning, which combines terminal outcome rewards with turn-level rubric evaluations and computes role-specific advantages for more precise credit assignment. Experiments on five long-horizon deep-search benchmarks such as BrowseComp and Xbench-DS show that IterSynth-8B achieves an average score of 50.7, surpassing the strongest prior $\leq$8B agent by +4.2\%. Moreover, IterSynth serves as a model-agnostic prompting paradigm, delivering substantial zero-shot gains over ReAct and similar prompting paradigms on frontier proprietary models.

</details>


### [15] [Automated Regulatory Compliance Question Answering in Financial Services with Domain-Adapted Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30009)
*Tobias Deußer,Abhishek Pillai,Aurelio F. Bariviera,Dhananjay Bhardwaj,Lorenz Sparrenberg,David Berghaus,Christian Bauckhage,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧凑模型在金融合规问答中的落地问题，通过分阶段领域适配检索器加RAFT-LoRA生成器提升性能，但发现生成质量的提升并不等同于可验证的事实落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构的规则手册密集且频繁修订，合规问答不仅要求语言流畅，还需要对权威文本的可验证落地。机构能在本地部署的通常是紧凑模型，而紧凑模型容易捏造义务，因此需要研究领域适配的检索增强生成流程能否弥补这一差距。

Method: 检索器基于LegalBERT分三阶段构建：蕴含调优，将问答-段落匹配重构为前提-假设重建；使用批内负样本的对比调优；与BM25进行分数级融合。生成器为2B-12B参数的紧凑模型，采用4比特量化部署，通过提示或使用LoRA的检索感知微调（RAFT）进行适配。在基于阿布扎比全球市场规则手册构建的ObliQA基准上评估。

Result: 分阶段检索器将Recall@10从0.256提升到0.774，优于BM25（0.678）和E5-large-v2（0.758）。RAFT-LoRA提升了所有可适配模型的RePASs综合答案质量得分，在最弱模型上增益最大。但适配后的模型无法迁移到澳大利亚判例法问题；完全无段落的闭卷模型得分与完整流程仅差0.011 RePASs，却引用为空并错误陈述义务。

Conclusion: 检索增益被直接测量，但生成增益只是RePASs分数上的提升，而非已证明的落地能力；落地本身需要一种RePASs无法提供的评估协议。

Abstract: Financial institutions operate under dense, frequently amended rulebooks, and answering a compliance question correctly requires not only fluency but verifiable grounding in the authoritative text. Large language models are attractive for this task, yet the models that firms can realistically deploy on-premise are compact ones, and compact models hallucinate obligations. We study whether a carefully domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation pipeline closes that gap. Our retriever is built in three stages on top of LegalBERT: entailment tuning that recasts question--passage matching as premise--hypothesis reconstruction, contrastive tuning with in-batch negatives, and score-level fusion with BM25. Our generator is a compact model (2B--12B parameters) served under 4-bit quantization, either prompted or adapted with retrieval-aware fine-tuning (RAFT) through LoRA. On ObliQA, a question-answering benchmark built from the Abu Dhabi Global Market rulebooks, the staged retriever raises Recall@10 from 0.256 to 0.774 and outperforms BM25 (0.678) and E5-large-v2 (0.758), the strongest general-purpose dense encoder we tested. RAFT-LoRA then improves the composite RePASs answer-quality score for every model we could adapt, with the largest gain on the weakest one. However, the adapted models do not transfer to Australian case-law questions, and a closed-book model that receives no passages at all scores within 0.011 RePASs of the full pipeline while producing answers that cite nothing and misstate obligations. The retrieval gain is therefore measured directly, the generation gain is a gain in RePASs rather than demonstrated grounding, and grounding itself requires an evaluation protocol that RePASs does not provide.

</details>


### [16] [Low-Cost Assays for Measuring Model Behavior Across Vendors and Releases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30012)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一套冻结、公开、跨厂商、低成本的行为stimulus测试框架，用三种转录解读方式（精确匹配、LLM评判员编码本、插桩环境）研究模型行为，在四年多代模型上发现趋同、抗性、立场稳定性与行为执行四类现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量语言模型行为很困难：需要在多模型、多提示、多版本上反复采样；行为多存于非结构化文本需先编码；结果还需足够严谨可读，才能有意义地比较模型与厂商。作者希望建立简单、廉价、可扩展、可复现的行为研究范式。

Method: 每项研究使用冻结的公开stimulus，在跨厂商模型面板上以相同方式运行，每模型成本仅数美元或更低。转录通过三种方式解读，按行为所需解释程度选择：(1) 对受限回复做精确匹配；(2) 由LLM评判员应用编码本，并逐码报告与人类编码者的一致性；(3) 插桩环境，独立于模型自述记录智能体实际行为。

Result: 在四年间前沿与开源实验室的模型版本上运行，发现四点：(1) 趋同——44个模型中有27个在被要求选词时四次尝试至少一次答"serendipity"；(2) 抗性——句尾加"right?"可使赞同度变化最多32个百分点，且符号随代际从谄媚翻转为抗拒，与标签表面形式相关；(3) 立场——模型在压力下是否坚持立场随代际变化，如何坚持则与所属实验室相关；(4) 执行——当被要求做与仓库文档相矛盾的事时，部分编码智能体从不默默服从、另一些总是服从，同一模型在不同harness下行为也会改变。

Conclusion: 这类随每次发布重跑的测试组合，可持续追踪模型行为在不同厂商与时间上的变化，为模型行为研究提供廉价、可扩展、可复现的标准化工具。

Abstract: Language models advise people, keep them company, and write software while they sleep. Measuring what they do is hard: behavior has to be sampled repeatedly across models, prompts and releases, most of it lives in unstructured text that has to be coded before it can be counted, and the result has to be legible and rigorous enough to meaningfully compare models and vendors. To address these constraints, we present a simple, cheap, scalable, and replicable model for studying model behavior. Each study is a frozen, public stimulus run identically on a cross-vendor panel, at a few dollars per model or less. Each reads its transcripts one of three ways, chosen by how much interpretation the behavior needs: exact match on a clamped reply, a codebook applied by LLM judges whose agreement with a human coder is reported per code, and an instrumented environment that records what an agent did independently of what it said. Run across four years of model releases from both frontier and open-source labs, these instruments find four things. Convergence: asked to pick a word, 27 of 44 models answer serendipity at least once in four tries. Resistance: a trailing "right?" moves endorsement by up to 32 points, and the sign flips from sycophantic to resistant as generations advance, keyed to the tag's surface form. House: whether a model holds a position under pressure tracks its generation, and how it holds tracks the lab that built it. Account: told to do something the documentation in their repository contradicts, some coding agents never went along silently and others always did, and the same model can change with the harness it runs in. Re-run on every release, batteries like these track how behavior is changing across vendors and over time.

</details>

### [17] [TW3Cast: A Frozen Router of Lightly Fine-Tuned Foundation Models for Time-Series Forecasting on GIFT-Eval, Selected Entirely on the Training Split](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28506)
*Nathan Thierry,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TW3Cast 是一个不含智能体与语言模型的时间序列预测系统，在 GIFT-Eval 基准上以平均 MASE 排名第 3（130 个条目中），通过冻结的路由表在 97 种配置上组合微调专家与基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 榜单前两名属于"智能体"类别（使用智能体或语言模型推理、生成或选择预测），而作者希望展示一种不使用智能体与语言模型、仅依靠一次性计算并冻结的路由表与轻微微调专家，也能取得接近顶尖的预测性能，从而验证避免复杂智能体机制是否足够。

Method: TW3Cast 的核心是一张在训练集上一次性计算后冻结的选型表。针对 97 种数据集、频率与预测长度组合，表格为每种配置指定四种模式之一：专门化模型（对 Chronos-2、TiRex 或 Toto 做 LoRA 或全参微调，训练数据经显式规则清洗与增强）、包含专门化模型的嵌入（quantile）融合、基线模型融合、或在训练集切出的回测上开展的选拔赛。所有决策均在回测上做出，专门化模型一旦在回测中胜过选拔赛即被采用，候选成本仅为数 MB 与数分钟 GPU 时间，失败候选不产生任何影响。三重防护机制用于抑制选择偏差：准确率与校准双重准则、对训练期见过该序列的候选施加非对称裕度、以及保守的逐窗口门控；选择规则本身也在时间元回测中确定。系统不使用任何智能体或语言模型，路由表、专家索引、固定的基线模型版本、提交分数文件与公开分数的日期快照均已发布。

Result: 最佳单基线模型单独使用的平均 MASE 排名为 33.8；在所有配置上使用选拔赛为 38.0；完整路由器达到 19.4，对应榜单第 3 名。论文中所有榜单数字均可由随发布文件的一个脚本复现。

Conclusion: 无需智能体或语言模型、仅通过冻结路由表与轻量微调专家，即可在 GIFT-Eval 上取得具竞争力的高排名，且方法成本低、可复现，并为选择偏差提供了多重防护。

Abstract: TW3Cast is a time-series forecasting system that reaches position 3 of 130 entries on the GIFT-Eval benchmark by mean MASE rank, as of 2026-09-14. The two entries above it belong to the leaderboard's agentic category, multi-step systems that use agents or language models to reason about, generate or select forecasts. TW3Cast runs no agent and no language model. Its selection is a table computed once on the training split and then frozen, and its experts are public foundation models lightly fine-tuned on those training splits. For each of the 97 dataset, frequency and horizon configurations, the table serves one of four modes: a specialist, which is a LoRA or full fine-tune of Chronos-2, TiRex or Toto whose training data was cleaned and enriched by explicit rules; a quantile blend that contains a specialist; a blend of base models; or a selection tournament played on a backtest carved from the training split. Every decision in the table was taken on that backtest. A specialist is admitted the moment it beats the tournament there, so a candidate costs a few megabytes and minutes of GPU time, and a failed candidate changes nothing. Three guarded mechanisms protect the selection from its own biases: a dual accuracy and calibration criterion, an asymmetric margin against candidates that saw the series during training, and conservative per-window gates. The selection rules themselves were chosen inside a temporal meta-backtest. The best base model served alone reaches a mean MASE rank of 33.8, the tournament served on every configuration reaches 38.0, and the full router reaches 19.4. The routing table, the expert index, the pinned base-model revisions, the submitted score file and the dated snapshot of the public scores are released, and every leaderboard number in this paper regenerates from them by one script.

</details>


### [18] [PAWS: Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28547)
*Tiviatis Sim,Jia Hui Woon,Xinming Gao,Chen Gao,Fengbin Zhu,Zheng Huanhuan,Chua Tat Seng,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAWS是一个政策驱动的智能体世界模拟数据集，覆盖36个美国金融经济政策事件、12,727条政策相关新闻和65,291条有来源依据的利益相关者行动，用于历史对齐的金融多智能体模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策干预通过公共传播、机构决策和利益相关者响应进行传导，但金融多智能体模拟数据集很少将这些过程与时序对齐的历史证据相连接，缺乏可审计、可复现的仿真基础。

Method: 构建PAWS数据集：将每条行动链接至支撑新闻，并用多层事件框架表示（交互模式、金融行动族与子类型、语义属性、对外部分类体系的条件映射）；实体归一化为标准组织，行动与每日市场收益上下文对齐；在2,522条分层抽样上由AI与人工独立评审并对分歧进行裁决；开展2008年卖空禁令与2001年小数化案例研究，并进行回放研究。

Result: 在2,522条分层行动样本上，独立AI与人工评审者在交互模式上达到89.4%的初始一致率；案例研究在密集与稀疏新闻场景下均恢复了政策时间线与相关市场模式；回放研究显示高准确率可能掩盖对稀有利益相关者行动的漏检。

Conclusion: PAWS为在历史锚定的金融模拟中评估智能体影响、政策响应级联和行动—结果对齐提供了可审计的基础，并指出行动时序与校准是核心挑战。

Abstract: Policy interventions propagate through public communication, institutional decisions, and stakeholder responses, yet datasets for financial multi-agent simulation rarely connect these processes to temporally aligned historical evidence. We introduce PAWS, a Policy-driven Agentic World Simulation dataset covering 36 verified U.S. financial and economic policy episodes, 12,727 policy-linked news records, and 65,291 source-grounded stakeholder actions. Each action is linked to its supporting news and represented by a multi-layer event frame capturing its interaction mode, financial-action family and subtype, semantic attributes, and conditional mappings to external taxonomies. Entities are resolved to normalized organizations, and actions are aligned with daily market-return context to support policy-agent simulation replay. On 2,522 stratified action samples, independent AI and human reviewers achieved 89.4% initial agreement on interaction mode, with disagreements subsequently adjudicated. Case studies of the 2008 short-selling ban and 2001 decimalization recover documented policy timelines and associated market patterns across both dense and sparse news settings. A replay study further shows that high accuracy can mask failure to detect rare stakeholder actions, identifying action timing and calibration as central challenges. PAWS provides an auditable substrate for evaluating agent influence, policy-response cascades, and action-outcome alignment in historically grounded financial simulations.

</details>


### [19] [Progressive Skill Discovery as Access Control for Tool-Using LLM Agents: Structural Governance through Role-Scoped Capability Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28693)
*Michael Stettler,Benjamin Girardet,Jonas Canton,Nicolas Corod*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 skilder 框架，将企业工具集封装为"角色"（技能、工具、指令及其约束的捆绑包），通过单一 MCP 服务器按需下发，实现确定性的策略合规与权限边界强制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在接入庞大企业工具集时难以安全扩展：全量工具暴露会导致上下文窗口过大、工具选择质量下降，且仅靠提示词定义的系统策略只是概率性建议而非硬约束；多智能体领域委派等现有方案又会分散审计日志，无法跨会话保证策略合规。

Method: 提出 skilder 框架，把能力打包为角色（技能、工具、指令及限制条件的集合）。智能体从最小角色目录起步，学习任务所需的角色，并通过单一 MCP 服务器获取每个角色的技能、指令与工具；由于工具只能随已学习的技能到达智能体，同一服务器可确定性地强制执行所学范围。在 13 项任务、6 个模型（每项 10 次运行）上与扁平上下文工具选择和multi-agent编排进行对比评估。

Result: 在模型完成发现流程并发起受治理调用的情况下，skilder 的模拟授权层成功强制了治理边界：未执行任何未授权工具调用或参数违规（如超支）。总体任务通过率还反映了各模型是否遵循发现协议及满足响应质量检查，这些失败并非授权失败。此外，智能体可在任务中途动态获取跨角色能力，从而在提供系统级硬性强制的同时保持问题求解灵活性。

Conclusion: skilder 通过角色化能力打包与单一 MCP 服务器的确定性作用域强制，兼顾了安全治理与跨角色动态能力获取的灵活性，为 LLM 智能体安全扩展企业工具集提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents struggle to scale safely when exposed to vast enterprise toolsets. Providing an agent with access to every internal tool leads to oversized context windows, degraded tool selection, and severe governance vulnerabilities - as system policies defined purely in prompts remain probabilistic advice rather than hard constraints. Existing mitigations, such as multi-agent domain delegation, decentralize audit logs and fail to guarantee policy compliance across sessions. We introduce skilder, a framework that packages capabilities into roles: bundles of skills, tools, and instructions, together with the limits that bound them. An agent begins with a minimal role catalog, learns the roles a task requires, and receives each role's skills, instructions, and tools through a single MCP server. Because tools reach the agent only inside learned skills, the same server enforces the scope of what was learned deterministically. We evaluate skilder against flat-context tool selection and multi-agent orchestration across 13 tasks using six models (10 runs each). Our results show that, when models completed discovery and issued a governed call, the skilder simulated authorization layer enforced governance boundaries: no unauthorized tool call or parameter violation (e.g., a spending-limit breach) executed. Aggregate task pass rates also reflect whether each model followed the discovery protocol and satisfied response-quality checks; those misses are not authorization failures. Furthermore, by allowing agents to dynamically acquire cross-role capabilities mid-task, skilder preserves problem-solving flexibility while providing hard system-level enforcement.

</details>


### [20] [BaseCamp --- An Agentic AI Framework for Automating DNA Sequencing Data Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28557)
*Eranga Bandara,Xueping Liang,Asanga Gunaratna,Tharaka Hewa,Abdul Rahman,Peter Foytik,Safdar H. Bouk,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Amin Hass,Wathsala Herath,Ross Gore,Ravi Mukkamala,Nihal Siriwardanagea,Gihan Siriwardanagea,Aruna Withanage,Nilaan Loganathan,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BaseCamp：面向DNA测序流水线决策层的agentic AI框架，用六个专用AI智能体在本地、人在回路中自动完成质控阈值选择、边界变异裁定、异常诊断与复核判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测序流水线的执行环节（质控、比对、变异检测、注释）已由工作流系统自动化，但其外围的"决策层"仍依赖人工：选择适应该样本与平台的质控阈值、裁定边界变异、诊断异常、决定哪些发现需专家复核。这些决策重复、依赖判断、操作者间不一致且常无记录。

Method: 提出BaseCamp agentic AI框架，将流水线分解为六个专用AI智能体（样本接收与质控、比对、变异检测、注释、跨阶段监控、报告）。智能体不执行序列分析，而由既有工具完成比对、检测与注释，智能体负责选择工具、配置参数、解释输出并决定后续动作；推理由一组微调的领域专用LLM组成，并由中心推理LLM协调，全部本地执行以保证测序数据不出环境，配合人在回路编排。

Result: 评估显示：智能体生成的配置与专家实践一致；显式过滤台账使原本无痕被过滤掉的内容可被审查；跨阶段异常检测能发现执行层监控遗漏的状况。

Conclusion: 将语言模型推理限制在可靠的判断层、同时保留既有工具的可复现性，BaseCamp为科学数据流水线的agentic自动化提供了可推广的蓝图。

Abstract: DNA sequencing pipelines, spanning quality control, alignment, variant calling, and annotation, are now reliably executed by workflow management systems that orchestrate established bioinformatics tools at scale. What remains manual is the decision layer surrounding that execution: selecting quality thresholds appropriate to a sample and platform, adjudicating borderline variant calls, diagnosing anomalies, and determining which findings warrant expert review. These decisions are repetitive, judgment-intensive, inconsistent across operators, and frequently undocumented. This paper introduces BaseCamp, a novel agentic AI framework for automating the decision layer of DNA sequencing pipelines. The framework decomposes the pipeline into six specialized AI agents, covering sample intake and quality control, alignment, variant calling, annotation, cross-stage monitoring, and reporting. Critically, BaseCamp agents do not perform sequence analysis: established tools execute alignment, calling, and annotation, while the agents select among them, configure them, interpret their output, and decide what follows. This confines language model reasoning to the judgment layer where it is reliable and preserves the reproducibility existing tooling guarantees. Agent reasoning is powered by a consortium of fine-tuned, domain-specialized large language models coordinated by a central reasoning LLM, executing locally so no sequencing data leaves the operating environment, under human-in-the-loop orchestration. Evaluation shows agent-generated configurations are concordant with expert practice, that an explicit filtering ledger renders inspectable what filtering otherwise removes without trace, and that cross-stage anomaly detection surfaces conditions execution monitoring misses. BaseCamp offers a generalizable blueprint for agentic automation of scientific data pipelines.

</details>


### [21] [AlphaDiverse: Post-Training Local Quantitative Research Agents for Diverse Exploration in Alpha Factor Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29014)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Zikun Wei,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaDiverse框架，通过多智能体多样化研究路径采集与本地智能体后训练，在无需外部API的情况下实现兼顾预测性能与探索多样性的alpha因子挖掘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统可实现alpha因子挖掘自动化，但依赖外部API导致成本、可用性与保密性受限；同时长研究循环容易反复利用少数成功经济机制，造成研究路径崩塌（research path collapse）。

Method: 构建包含多智能体alpha研究系统、多样化研究路径收集与本地智能体后训练三部分的框架：让研究系统生成互补的计划组合，并在循环中变化研究环境以采集多样路径；用这些多样化轨迹对本地Planner与Realizer智能体进行监督微调热启动；再提出联合GRPO方法，以预测质量与贡献多样性为目标同时优化两者；研究反馈限定在内层时间段数据，冻结的最终模型在外层后期数据上评估以避免测试集调参。

Result: 在四个中国股票市场的实验中，AlphaDiverse能够同时实现有竞争力的预测性能与更广泛的探索。

Conclusion: 将多智能体研究、多样化路径采集与本地化后训练结合，可缓解对外部API的依赖与研究路径崩塌问题，在本地可部署的alpha因子挖掘中兼顾预测质量与探索多样性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems can automate alpha factor mining, but their reliance on external APIs limits control over cost, availability, and confidentiality. Long research loops also tend to revisit a few successful economic mechanisms that lead to research path collapse. To address these limitations, we propose AlphaDiverse, a framework that integrates a multi-agent alpha research system, diverse research path collection, and post-training for local agents. We let the research system generate complementary plan portfolios and vary research environments across loops to collect diverse research paths. Using these diverse traces, we warm-start local Planner and Realizer agents with supervised fine-tuning. Then, we propose a joint GRPO method to optimize both of them using predictive quality and diversity of contributions. Research feedback is confined to inner period data, while a frozen final model is evaluated on a later outer period data, thereby avoiding test-set tuning. Experiments across four Chinese stock universes show that AlphaDiverse can combine competitive prediction with broader exploration.

</details>


### [22] [DEEPO: Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization for Hallucination in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28570)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Miao Pan,Wangjie Gan,Jingxiao Yang,Fan Wang,Weiming Liu,Cheng Tan,Xuhong Zhang,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DEEPO方法，通过双阶段增强（信号方差正则化+梯度预条件）改善多模态大模型强化学习中幻觉修正链条的两个薄弱环节，有效降低幻觉并保持准确率与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习被广泛用于提升多模态大模型的推理能力，但其对幻觉的影响并不稳定。作者将其归因于"修正链条"（从奖励到参数更新）中的两个薄弱点：一是在rollout层面，高语义熵的困难查询常产生"一致错误"的样本组，使组相对优势塌缩为零，而这正是幻觉风险最高之处；二是在优化层面，自信但错误的token在梯度上不可见——随着类别策略分布变尖，期望得分梯度范数消失，导致最需要修正的预测反而获得最弱的更新。

Method: 提出双熵增强策略优化（DEEPO），包含两个阶段：（1）信号方差正则化：以语义熵触发专家前缀，在高不确定性查询上注入有依据的续写，提供直接监督并恢复优势方差；（2）梯度预条件：采用优势符号感知的Renyi预条件，抵消logit层面的饱和，使修正能够作用于操作置信区间内的自信错误。

Result: 两个分支单独使用均优于GRPO；二者的交互作用在VideoMMMU（评测中最复杂的长时程任务）上统计显著（+4.0，95%置信区间[1.1, 6.9]），在其他任务上呈可加性。DEEPO在降低幻觉的同时保持了准确率与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: DEEPO通过针对rollout与优化两个层面的双阶段增强，有效解决了MLLM强化学习中幻觉修正不均衡的问题，在保持准确率和训练稳定性的前提下显著降低幻觉，尤其在复杂长时程任务上表现出显著增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used to sharpen reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet its effect on hallucination is uneven. We trace this to two weak points in the \emph{correction chain} from reward to parameter update. At the rollout level, hard queries---those with high semantic entropy---frequently produce unanimously wrong sample groups, collapsing the group-relative
  advantage to zero exactly where hallucination risk is highest. At the optimization level, confident-but-wrong tokens are gradient-invisible: a categorical policy's expected score-gradient norm vanishes as its distribution sharpens, so the predictions that most need correction receive the weakest updates. We propose Dual-Entropy Enhanced Policy Optimization (DEEPO), a dual-stage enhancement combining signal
  variance regularization with gradient preconditioning: semantic-entropy-triggered expert prefixes inject grounded continuations on high-uncertainty queries, providing direct supervision and restoring advantage variance, while advantage-sign-aware Renyi preconditioning counteracts logit-level saturation so correction reaches confident errors in the operational confidence regime. Both branches improve over GRPO individually; their interaction is statistically significant on VideoMMMU---the most complex long-horizon task in our evaluation suite (+4.0$, 95\% CI [1.1, 6.9])---and additive elsewhere. DEEPO reduces hallucination while preserving accuracy and training stability.

</details>


### [23] [Evaluation of Multi-Turn Consistency in LLM Agents: Survival Analysis and Failure-Rationale Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29508)
*Igor Bogdanov,Olga Manakina,Chung-Horng Lung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在受控的20步多智能体延迟满足场景中评估LLM智能体的时序一致性，通过84,540条轨迹的生存分析与推理文本分类，揭示了随时间和情境变化的"失败模式"及推理一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在孤立任务上表现良好，但在长时间交互中容易出现行为不一致，现有评估缺乏对多轮长时序可靠性的系统刻画，因此需要引入延迟满足范式来量化失败风险随时间的变化。

Method: 构建20步多智能体延迟满足实验：每步智能体选择继续延迟奖励或立即索取（终止回合）；全因子操纵社会可见性（私密/公开）、人格压力源与审议策略，运行84,540条轨迹覆盖8个模型家族。将首次索取奖励视为时间-事件结果，估计Kaplan-Meier生存曲线并拟合离散时间风险回归；同时基于13,780条终止回合的审议文本，用LLM辅助标注加人工审核（κ=0.83）建立七类推理分类体系。

Result: 推理类型随时间和情境系统性变化：早期失败更多由冲动驱动，后期失败更多表现为疲劳与成本收益权衡，公开情境下规范性理由增加。在失败案例中，审议越长，推理内部矛盾（同时支持延迟与索取）比例越高，挑战了"推理文本越多越一致"的假设。生存分析与推理分析共同揭示了不同的时序可靠性阶段和模型特有的"失败指纹"。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种评估多轮智能体行为不一致性的诊断视角，表明仅靠任务成功率不足以衡量时序可靠性，需要结合生存分析与推理语言分析来识别失败模式，为LLM智能体长时交互评估提供新框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents may perform well on isolated tasks yet drift into inconsistency over extended interaction. We evaluate temporal consistency in a controlled 20-step multi-agent setting inspired by delayed-gratification studies. At each step, an agent chooses between continuing to delay a reward or claiming it immediately (terminating the episode). Across a full-factorial manipulation of social visibility (private vs public), persona stressors, and deliberation policy, we run 84,540 trajectories spanning 8 model families. Treating the first reward-claim as a time-to-event outcome, we estimate Kaplan-Meier survival curves and fit discrete-time hazard regression to quantify how experimental factors shift failure risk over time. Then, to analyze rationales and language patterns associated with failure, we build a seven-category taxonomy from 13,780 deliberation traces from agents who choose to terminate the episode, using an LLM-assisted labeling paired with human audit ($κ=0.83$). Rationale profiles change systematically with time and context: early failures are more impulse-driven, later failures more fatigue- and cost-benefit-framed, while public settings increase norm-oriented justifications. We also find a deliberation-inconsistency association: among failures, longer deliberation correlates with higher rates of intra-rationale contradiction (simultaneous pro-delay and pro-claim statements), challenging the assumption that more reasoning text implies greater consistency. Together, the survival and rationale analyses reveal distinct temporal reliability regimes and model-specific "failure fingerprints", offering an evaluation lens for diagnosing inconsistency in multi-turn agent behavior.

</details>


### [24] [TWIST: A Proposed Benchmark for Intervention Quality in Conversational Memory, with a Human-Validated Draft-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28575)
*Subrat Panda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TWIST基准，评测长对话记忆系统在信念变化点的“干预质量”，并与召回指标配对使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长对话记忆基准多测召回和提示性知识更新，但未衡量系统是否在正确的时机介入，如检测张力、审查草稿、保留被取代历史等。补上“干预质量”这一未被测量的属性可避免仅靠召回分数掩盖误报与漏报的权衡。

Method: TWIST扩展LoCoMo语料与评测框架，设四条轨迹：无提示张力检测、对出站草稿与记录的一致性审查、用当前信念作答并保留取代历史、敏感召回治理。每个检测/阻断指标都配同表面的“不应过度检测”硬负例对照。基准先经独立双盲标注与仲裁、判官诱饵校准、可分性审查验证。在人工验证的Track B v1.0（161项，仲裁后kappa=0.85）上，用13种配置基线阶梯定位原因。

Result: 无配置能同时实现高矛盾召回、高硬负例特异性和高归因：flat-RAG基线召回真矛盾0.76-0.97，但误报同表面安全草稿16%-43%；面向连贯性的部署系统特异性0.98-1.00，却仅捕获42%真矛盾。金标矛盾单独从证据即可检测（召回1.000），校准模型在给定完整对话时几乎解决该轨迹，提示检索覆盖存在较大缺口；仅草稿基线显示模型相关的风格先验。

Conclusion: TWIST画像与召回分数并列，用以衡量记忆系统是否知道何时介入、何时不介入，揭示了召回单一指标无法看到的干预权衡。

Abstract: Long-conversation memory benchmarks increasingly test recall and prompted knowledge updates, and recent work studies evolving user beliefs and memory state. TWIST is a proposed benchmark suite for a complementary, unmeasured property: intervention quality -- whether a deployed memory system, exercised through its own ingest/recall/vet surface, acts correctly at belief change points. Four tracks cover unprompted tension detection, vetting outgoing drafts against the record, answering with current beliefs while preserving supersession history, and governing sensitive recall. The suite extends LoCoMo's corpora and harness, pairing every detect/block metric with a matched do-not-over-detect control: surface-matched hard negatives price false intervention, so no track can be gamed by flagging everything. The benchmark itself is validated first: independent, gold-blind double annotation with adjudication, judge decoy calibration, and a separability audit. On the human-validated Track B v1.0 key (161 items, post-adjudication kappa = 0.85), no tested configuration simultaneously achieves high contradiction recall, high hard-negative specificity, and high attribution: flat-RAG baselines detect 0.76-0.97 of true contradictions but falsely flag 16-43% of surface-matched safe drafts depending on backend, while a deployed coherence-oriented system almost never over-flags (0.98-1.00 specificity) yet catches 42% of true contradictions -- a trade-off no recall-only score can see. A 13-configuration baseline ladder localizes causes: every gold contradiction is detectable from its evidence alone (recall 1.000), calibrated models nearly solve the track given the full transcript -- consistent with substantial retrieval-coverage gaps -- and draft-only floors reveal model-dependent style priors. A system's TWIST profile, beside its recall score, measures whether memory knows when to intervene and when not to.

</details>


### [25] [Adversarial Closed-Loop Curriculum for Evolving Role-Playing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28609)
*Zheng Zhang,Liu Liu,Qi Chai,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Mao Zheng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AdvRole，将角色扮演强化学习转化为对抗式闭环课程，通过协同训练 Actor 与 Rewriter 动态生成难样本，持续针对 Actor 薄弱区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的角色扮演智能体 RL 方法通常在固定场景池上训练，随智能体能力提升，其薄弱场景不断变化，而训练分布保持静态，形成分布瓶颈，限制进一步提升。

Method: AdvRole 交替训练一个学习角色扮演的 Actor 和一个 Rewriter；Rewriter 将角色设定与对话上下文改写为针对 Actor 的困难场景，并以"性能差距奖励"训练，即偏好相比原场景更能降低当前 Actor 得分的改写，使场景池随 Actor 演化。

Result: 在三个涵盖中英文的角色扮演基准及新发布的多语言基准上，AdvRole 一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 对抗式上下文改写可有效构建随 Actor 能力演化的闭环课程，持续定位并攻坚角色—上下文空间中未掌握区域，提升角色扮演智能体的泛化与水平。

Abstract: Role-playing agents based on large language models have been widely applied in areas such as personalized assistance and social simulation. Recent RL methods typically train on a fixed scenario pool collected before learning begins. This creates a distributional bottleneck: as the agent improves, the scenarios where it performs poorly also change, while the training distribution remains static. Therefore, we propose AdvRole, an adversarial context rewriting framework that turns role-playing RL into a closed-loop curriculum. AdvRole alternates between an Actor that learns to role-play and a Rewriter that edits character profiles and dialogue contexts into actor-specific hard scenarios. The Rewriter is trained with a performance-gap reward, which favors rewrites that reduce the current Actor's score relative to the original scenario. As a result, the scenario pool evolves with the Actor and continuously targets under-mastered regions of the character-context space. Experiments on three role-playing benchmarks covering English and Chinese, as well as a new multilingual benchmark we release, show that AdvRole consistently outperforms baselines.

</details>


### [26] [GRASP: Generating, Revising, and Assessing for Strategic Planning with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30147)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRASP：一种策略感知的多阶段规划框架，通过模块化解耦提升LLM复杂任务规划可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在任务复杂度上升时可靠性下降，难以生成高质量可执行的自然语言规划，尤其在多任务场景下性能急剧崩溃。

Method: GRASP将规划流程解耦为上下文隔离的专门模块：GenPlan预编译全局宏观准则，RevPlan在独立上下文中探索局部替代策略，VerPlan用多准则判别器独立评估轨迹。

Result: 在Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling（约+12.4%）、ZebraLogic（约+30.8%）和SciBench Math上取得新SOTA；多任务扩展下消除性能退化惩罚，交错双任务绝对提升达16.7%，并超越GPT-5-mini约14.5%。

Conclusion: 通过上下文隔离与严格宏观正则化，GRASP显著缓解复杂与多任务规划的性能退化，优于直接LLM规划器及前沿推理模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) typically exhibit a performance profile where reliability degrades as task complexity increases. We address the challenge of generating high-quality natural language executable plans for complex tasks by introducing $\textbf{GRASP}$, a strategy-aware, multi-stage planning framework. GRASP decouples the planning pipeline across specialized, context-isolated modules: it pre-compiles global macro-guidelines (GenPlan), explores alternative localized strategies within isolated context windows (RevPlan), and independently evaluates trajectories using a multi-criteria discriminator (VerPlan). Empirical evaluations show that GRASP consistently establishes a new state-of-the-art frontier across diverse datasets, yielding substantial accuracy gains over direct LLM planners on Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling ($\sim$12.4$\%$$\uparrow$), ZebraLogic ($\sim$30.8$\%$$\uparrow$), and SciBench Math. Crucially, under multi-task scaling-where standard planners suffer immediate performance collapse-GRASP completely flattens the multi-task degradation penalty. In interleaved dual-task environments, GRASP achieves an absolute accuracy gain of up to 16.7$\%$ over direct LLM planners. Furthermore, by isolating context and enforcing strict macro-regularization, GRASP outperforms frontier reasoning models (such as GPT-5-mini) by a margin of 14.5$\%$.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Surface Style: Aligning Multi-Turn User Simulators with Behavioral Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28690)
*Geng Chen,Ruotong Pan,Zhirui Yang,Qiqi He,Jiawei Chen,Zhang Yunfei,Chongyuan Chen,Minxuan Lv,Zheng Yang,Win-Bin Huang,Xiangyu Wu,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACER多轮用户模拟器，显式建模用户演化意图并对齐真实交互轨迹，两阶段训练（SFT+多轮RL），在真实客服会话上显著超越基线，并构建动态营销基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建、评估和改进大规模交互式AI依赖忠实的用户模拟，但生成看似合理的个体回复并不能保证模拟用户复现真实交互中的意图演化与结果。因此需要显式建模用户意图演化并与真实交互轨迹对齐。

Method: TRACER采用两阶段训练：先对真实用户对话进行监督微调，再进行多轮强化学习。RL阶段结合分层的结果级与轨迹级奖励，以及偏差感知的优势调制，缓解长对话中的奖励稀疏与信用分配问题。基于该模拟器进一步提出动态营销基准，通过模拟交互联合评估LLM的说服效果与回复质量。

Result: 在组织为参考队列的真实客服会话上，TRACER-7B在转化F1上超越最强基线11.4，同时取得最低的群体级转化率误差与语义轨迹距离，并能泛化到分布外场景。人类图灵测试识别准确率接近随机水平，表明生成对话具有自然性。基准实验揭示更高的回复质量并不必然对应更高的转化率。

Conclusion: TRACER通过显式建模意图演化并对齐真实轨迹，实现了更忠实的多轮用户模拟，且其衍生的动态营销基准表明回复质量与说服转化效果并非一致，为交互式AI的评估提供了更贴近真实结果的手段。

Abstract: Faithful user simulation is fundamental to building, evaluating, and improving interactive AI at scale. However, plausible individual responses do not ensure that simulated users reproduce the intent evolution and outcomes observed in real interactions. We propose TRACER, a multi-turn user simulator that explicitly models users' evolving intent and learns to align simulated behavior with real interaction trajectories. TRACER is trained in two stages: supervised fine-tuning on real user dialogues, followed by multi-turn reinforcement learning. The RL stage combines hierarchical outcome- and trajectory-level rewards with deviation-aware advantage modulation, jointly mitigating reward sparsity and credit assignment in long dialogues. On real customer-service sessions organized into reference cohorts, TRACER-7B surpasses the strongest baseline by 11.4 conversion F1, while also achieving the lowest group-level conversion-rate error and semantic trajectory distance, and generalizing to out-of-distribution scenarios. Human Turing tests yield identification accuracy close to chance, supporting the perceived naturalness of generated conversations. Building on this simulator, we further introduce the Dynamic Marketing Benchmark, which jointly evaluates persuasion effectiveness and response quality of LLMs through simulated interactions, revealing that higher response quality does not necessarily correspond to higher conversion rates.

</details>


### [28] [Driving Epidemic Models with AI Agents: the Epydemix Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28692)
*Nicolò Gozzi,Ciro Cattuto,Alessandro Vespignani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Epydemix Agent Framework，在开源随机传染病建模库 Epydemix 之上增加加成层，使基于大语言模型的智能体能够通过自然语言完成建模全流程，并保持可审计与可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体虽能为科学软件提供便捷的自然语言接口，但可靠性并不自动得到保证，因此需要专门框架来约束和支撑智能体的建模行为。

Method: 框架为 Epydemix 增加四项能力：可用模型与参数的发现、声明式场景规格的预防性验证、通过经测试的库代码执行、以及结果的可审查性。每一步都读取输入文件并将结果保存为独立输出包。

Result: 通过一个比较新型呼吸道病毒疫苗接种策略的端到端案例展示完整流程；并在 50 次智能体会话与五项建模任务中，将使用该框架与直接使用 Python 接口进行对比，发现在多数任务上减少了交互轮次、输出 token 与成本，除非以资源换取逐点可复现性。

Conclusion: 该框架可在无需编写自定义代码的情况下支持智能体完成从自然语言场景描述到定量结果、图表与结果解读的全过程，并使流程可审计、可复现，同时通常提升效率。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence agents based on large language models provide convenient natural language interfaces to scientific software, but reliability is not automatic. Here we introduce the Epydemix Agent Framework, an additive layer over Epydemix, an open-source Python library for stochastic compartmental epidemic modeling. The framework extends the library with four capabilities to facilitate interaction with an AI agent: discovery of available models and parameters, preventive validation of a declarative scenario specification, execution through tested library code, and inspectability of results. These capabilities let an agent handle the entire modeling process, from the natural-language description of the scenario to quantitative results, figures, and interpretation of findings without writing custom code. Each step reads input files and saves results in a separate output bundle, making the process auditable and reproducible. First, we show the end-to-end workflow with a case study comparing vaccination strategies for a novel respiratory virus. Second, we assessed the framework across 50 agent sessions and five modeling tasks by comparing the agent use of the framework against the direct use of the Python interface. The framework reduced turns, output tokens, and cost on most tasks, unless it trades resources for per-point reproducibility.

</details>


### [29] [Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Small Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28765)
*Gaurisankar Jayadas,Aske Plaat,Álvaro Serra-Gómez,Sandheep P*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在0.8B小模型上验证"推理+搜索"RLVR配方，无需蒸馏即可将七基准平均精确匹配从0.092提升至0.352（3.8倍），但奖励形状影响显著，仅精确匹配的稀疏奖励表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在数学、代码等奖励明确的任务上表现良好，但在奖励不明确的任务（如开放域问答）上是否有效仍存疑。已有的"推理+搜索"配方仅在大模型上验证，1B以下参数规模仅有依赖大模型蒸馏的版本，缺乏纯小模型自主训练的证据。

Method: 使用Qwen3.5-0.8B，通过GRPO结合交错的维基百科搜索工具，在MuSiQue数据集上训练；仅改变奖励形状（三种）并各跑三个随机种子；对每个checkpoint在七个问答基准组成的留出测试集上评估，训练循环中不含蒸馏步骤。

Result: 配方有效：最佳运行达到0.352平均精确匹配，对比未训练下限0.092，提升3.8倍。奖励形状同样重要：忠实于Search-R1的"仅精确匹配"奖励在三个种子上、在匹配训练步数下均为三者中最差，甚至在其直接优化的精确匹配指标上也是最差。

Conclusion: 稀疏精确匹配奖励（数学与代码中RLVR的默认选择）不适合此规模模型作为起点。"推理+搜索"场景可为小模型RLVR提供合适的奖励，但小模型RLVR需要自身的奖励设计研究，而非大模型配方的缩小版复制。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) performs well on problems with clear rewards, such as mathematics and coding, but whether it also works where the reward is less clear remains open. The reason-over-search recipe applies RLVR to open-domain question answering, where retrieval grounds the answer and a match against the reference supplies the reward. So far it has been demonstrated on large models, and below one billion parameters only with distillation from a larger teacher. We test the recipe on a small model. We train Qwen3.5-0.8B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and an interleaved Wikipedia-search tool on MuSiQue, varying only the reward across three shapes over three seeds each, and we evaluate every checkpoint held-out on a seven-benchmark question-answering suite. The recipe works: the best run reaches 0.352 average exact match against a 0.092 untrained floor, a 3.8-fold gain, with no distillation step in the training loop. The reward shape also matters. The Search-R1-faithful exact-match-only reward is the worst of the three at every seed at the matched training horizon, and it is worst even on exact match, the metric it directly optimises. We conclude that the sparse exact-match reward, RLVR's default in mathematics and code, is the wrong starting point for models of this size. The reason-over-search setting can supply a suitable reward for RLVR on small models, but small-model RLVR needs its own reward-design study rather than a scaled-down copy of a large-model recipe.

</details>


### [30] [Agent Memory with Episodic Retrieval for Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28771)
*Nuoyue Xu,Jiang Liu,Wenxuan Huang,Xiang Zhang,Juntai Cao,Jiaqi Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出META——首个类RAG的情景记忆增强多智能体交易框架，通过指标智能体+决策智能体+记忆模块提升短周期交易的方向准确率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体交易框架要么侧重长时段预测，要么是静态分析器（无状态），难以适应复杂交易场景的实时决策需求，缺乏对历史经验的复用与市场状态自适应能力。

Method: META集成一组专门化指标智能体（Trend、MACD、Stochastic、RSI、SMA、AVWAP、Heikin-Ashi），由一个决策智能体融合各指标报告；并引入记忆模块，将过往交易情景编码为市场状态嵌入（含结果与反思）进行检索与更新，从而在相似市场状态下召回相关经验并自适应重加权信号。

Result: 在短周期评估下，META实现了更高的方向准确率与更强的鲁棒性，表明情景记忆能有效支撑状态感知、可解释且低延迟的交易决策。

Conclusion: 情景记忆为交易与决策提供了一种强有力的机制，可兼顾状态自适应、可解释性与低延迟；项目代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in financial analysis and reasoning, inspiring recent advances in agent-based trading frameworks. While these systems show promise, prior approaches either emphasize long-horizon forecasting or operate as stateless analyzers, limiting their applicability to the demands of trading in complicated settings. To address these gaps, we introduce META (Memory Enhanced Trading Agent), the first RAG-like episodic-memory-augmented multi-agent framework for financial decision making. META integrates a family of specialized indicator agents (e.g., Trend, MACD, Stochastic, RSI, SMA, AVWAP, Heikin-Ashi) with a Decision Agent that fuses their reports, and a Memory module that retrieves and updates past trading episodes encoded as market state embeddings with outcomes and reflections. By recalling relevant experiences and adaptively reweighting signals under similar market regimes, META achieves improved directional accuracy and robustness under short-horizon evaluation. Our results demonstrate that episodic memory provides a powerful mechanism for regime-aware, interpretable, and low-latency decision-making in trading and decision making. The code of this project is released on GitHub.

</details>


### [31] [RECLAIM: Can Agents Reproduce the Claims of Machine Learning Papers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28850)
*Mithil Salunkhe,Haochen Ding,Samridhi Verma,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RECLAIM基准，用100篇NeurIPS 2025论文评测AI代理复现机器学习实验的能力，按作者公开资源的多少划分难度层级，并由独立语言模型评分，最好代理在Run层仅复现41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复现机器学习论文涉及安装软件、调试、运行实验等大量研究步骤，而这正是AI代理日益承担的工作，但缺乏标准化、可年度更新的评测基准来衡量代理的复现能力。

Method: 构建RECLAIM基准，包含100篇NeurIPS 2025论文，可每年从新会议重建；为每篇论文预先固定要复现的结果、成功判定标准和GPU小时预算；依据作者公开内容划分难度层级：Run层有代码、数据和权重，Retrain层缺权重需代理训练模型，Reimplement层缺代码需代理自行编写；由独立的语言模型根据日志和输出而非代理自报进行评分；对每篇论文运行四个代理各一次。

Result: 各层级最佳代理仅能复现Run层41%的论文，Retrain层27%，Reimplement层15%且表现最差；失败尝试平均只使用了29%的预算，多数在预算未用尽时即停止；最常见的代理错误是编写方法时未对照论文数值进行任何检查，400次运行中出现63次。

Conclusion: AI代理的论文复现能力仍显著不足，资源公开程度越低表现越差，且失败多源于过早放弃和缺乏与论文数值的核对，RECLAIM为持续追踪该能力提供了可年度更新的评测框架。

Abstract: Reproducing a machine learning paper involves most research steps, from installing software and debugging to running experiments, work that AI agents increasingly do. We introduce RECLAIM, a benchmark of 100 NeurIPS 2025 papers that can be rebuilt yearly from new conferences. For each paper we fix in advance the result to reproduce, what counts as a successful reproduction, and a GPU-hour budget. An agent must reproduce that result using the paper and whatever its authors released. What the authors released decides the difficulty tier. Run-tier releases include code, data, and weights; Retrain-tier releases lack weights, so the agent trains the model; Reimplement-tier releases lack code, so the agent writes it. A separate language model grades runs from logs and outputs rather than agents' reports. We run four agents once per paper; the best agent in each tier reproduces only 41% of Run-tier papers, 27% at Retrain, and 15% at Reimplement, where every agent does worst. Failed attempts use on average 29% of their budget, so most stop with budget left. The most common agent error is writing the method without checking any part against the paper's numbers, in 63 of 400 runs.

</details>


### [32] [Human-AI-Powered Hypothesis Testing: Cost-Aware Selective AI Scoring and Sequential Human Escalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28859)
*Dae Woong,Ham,Xuejun Zhao,Stefanus Jasin,Fenghua Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何将AI判断与选择性人工核验结合，以最小成本进行有效的假设检验，提出SCALE策略并给出信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多被用作低成本评判者来评估输出、标注数据和判断系统是否达标。但AI判断可能有偏或含噪，将其直接当作真实标签用于正式统计推断并不严谨，因为严格的假设检验需要显式控制第一类和第二类错误。因此需要研究如何在保证检验有效性的同时以最低成本利用AI判断。

Method: 论文考虑一个具有隐藏二元标签的样本总体。在选定固定样本池后，决策者可以选择性查询AI、直接将样本送人工、在观察到AI报告后将AI评分样本升级给人工，或在证据充分时停止。作者推导了达到规定检验错误所需最小成本的信息论下界，并通过依赖报告的信息前沿刻画AI信息与人工核验的价值。基于该刻画，提出SCALE——一种顺序成本感知策略，结合选择性AI评分与自适应人工升级；并扩展到AI输出模型未知、使用配对AI-人工试点数据的情形。

Result: SCALE在有限样本下有效，且当目标错误概率趋于零时一阶匹配下界。数值上，当某一信息源明显占优时，SCALE趋近于纯人工或纯AI检验；而当低成本AI判断与选择性人工核验都很有价值时，SCALE实现最大节省。

Conclusion: 该工作为在正式统计假设检验中使用AI判断提供了有理论保证、成本最优的框架，说明兼顾低成本AI评分与选择性人工核验可在控制错误的前提下显著降低成本。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as inexpensive judges to evaluate outputs, label data, and assess whether a system meets a desired quality standard. Yet using AI judgments for formal statistical inference is fundamentally different from simply treating them as ground-truth labels: AI evaluations can be biased or noisy, and rigorous hypothesis testing requires explicit control of type-I and type-II errors. We study how to use AI judgments, together with selective human verification, to conduct a valid hypothesis test at minimum cost. We consider a population of items with hidden binary labels. After choosing a fixed pool of items, the decision maker can selectively query AI, send an item directly to a human, escalate an AI-scored item to a human after observing the AI report, or stop once sufficient evidence has accumulated. We derive an information-theoretic lower bound that captures the minimum cost of achieving prescribed testing errors and characterizes the value of AI information and human verification through a report-dependent information frontier. Motivated by this characterization, we develop SCALE, a sequential cost-aware policy that combines selective AI scoring with adaptive human escalation. SCALE is valid at finite sample sizes and matches the lower bound to first order as the target error probabilities vanish. We further extend the framework to an unknown AI-output model using paired AI-human pilot data. Numerically, SCALE approaches Human-only or AI-only testing when one source clearly dominates, while achieving its largest savings when inexpensive AI judgments and selective human verification are both valuable.

</details>


### [33] [PFArena: Benchmarking Language Models for Protein Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28921)
*Yawen Ouyang,Xinbo Zhang,Ziyuan Ma,Yixin Wu,Wenbin Liao,Feiran Zhang,Wenjie Li,Lihao Wang,Hao Wang,Xiaoqing Zheng,Xuefeng Yan,Lei Bai,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shuyi Zhang,Wei-Ying Ma,Dahua Lin,Bowen Zhou,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出PFArena基准，系统评测蛋白质语言模型、大语言模型及LLM智能体在蛋白质改造任务中的相对表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质改造需要探索巨大的序列空间，但湿实验验证通量低、成本高。尽管PLM、LLM及LLM智能体已被尝试用于蛋白质改造，但在贴近真实实验决策情境下，它们的相对效能尚不明确，缺乏统一评测基准。

Method: 构建PFArena基准，包含四个受控任务接口，覆盖单突变体生成与多突变体排序；通过提供不同程度的突变适应度数据，模拟四类具有不同先验实验背景的研究场景。评测对象包括6个PLM、6个LLM和5个基于LLM的智能体，并采用互补指标同时衡量峰值与整体改造表现。

Result: 模型表现随目标特异性实验证据的可用性而系统性变化：PLM凭借蛋白质先验在开放式单突变生成中表现较好；LLM与智能体在多突变体排序中表现更强，尤其在具备目标特异性适应度数据时。但随着搜索空间增大和突变深度增加，所有模型家族都面临根本性挑战。

Conclusion: PFArena揭示了不同模型范式在蛋白质改造中的互补优势与共同瓶颈，为模型辅助蛋白质改造的可复现研究提供了代码与基准套件。

Abstract: Protein modification requires navigating an immense sequence space, yet wet-lab validation remains low-throughput and costly. Although computational paradigms including protein language models (PLMs), large language models (LLMs), and LLM-based agents have shown promise in protein modification, their relative efficacy across realistic experimental decision-making settings remains unclear. To bridge this gap, we introduce PFArena, a benchmark comprising four controlled task interfaces that cover single-mutant generation and multi-mutant ranking. By providing varying levels of mutation fitness data, PFArena reflects four representative research scenarios characterized by differing degrees of prior experimental context. We assess six PLMs, six LLMs, and five LLM-based agents using complementary metrics to measure both peak and overall protein modification performance.
