A universal Turing machine gives us a search space over all computable data-generating processes, imposing little domain-specific structure, and self-play searches over this space for useful training data. We test whether zero-shot performance on natural data improves predictably with self-play compute; this is a clean test of transfer since neither generator nor learner is trained on natural data. Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss exhibits predictable scaling in compute. The models also exhibit in-context learning, and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training.

</details>


### [100] [PrivDrift: Auditing User-Secret Leakage Under Topic Drift in Active LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30094)
*Luciano Maldonado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PrivDrift 基准，用于审计用户在多轮对话中披露的秘密信息在话题漂移与说服性探测后是否仍可被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益作为持久助手运行于共享会话和工具增强场景中，用户披露的敏感信息可能在对话转向无关话题后仍被后续提示行为性地恢复，而现有隐私评估多聚焦训练数据记忆或即时越狱，缺乏对活跃对话中持续泄漏风险的评测。

Method: 构建 PrivDrift 基准，包含 1,000 组受控多轮对话，嵌入预置秘密、内容密集的话题漂移轮次以及标准化提取探测，在三个具备扩展上下文窗口的 LLM 上测试话题漂移与说服强度对秘密可恢复性的影响。

Result: 对话级混合泄漏率显著，介于 38.7% 至 54.6% 之间，并随模型、秘密类型和说服强度大幅变化；在测试的漂移窗口内，额外话题漂移并未可靠降低泄漏。

Conclusion: 活跃 LLM 场景中的隐私风险应被视为一种持续性的行为失效模式，而非仅归因于训练数据记忆或即时越狱行为。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as persistent assistants in user-facing, shared-session, and tool-augmented settings. When users disclose sensitive information during an active conversation, that information may remain behaviorally recoverable through later prompts even after the dialogue shifts to unrelated topics. We introduce \textbf{PrivDrift}, a benchmark for auditing whether user-disclosed secrets remain recoverable after conversational topic drift and persuasion-based probing. PrivDrift contains 1{,}000 controlled multi-turn dialogues with seeded secrets, content-dense drift turns, and standardized extraction probes. Across three LLMs with extended context windows, dialogue-level hybrid leakage remains substantial, ranging from 38.7\% to 54.6\%, and varies strongly by model, secret type, and persuasion intensity. Within the tested drift window, additional topic drift does not reliably reduce leakage, suggesting that privacy risk in active LLM contexts should be evaluated as a persistent behavioral failure mode rather than only as training-data memorization or immediate jailbreak behavior.

</details>


### [101] [HEXIS: Compiling Skills into Extended Finite State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30123)
*Minghao LI*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HEXIS 将智能体技能编译为扩展有限状态机，把技能知识与控制流分离，在四个基准和四种执行器上平均超过 Skill + ReAct 16.1 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能虽可复用，但需要反复推断如何应用及下一步操作，导致任务推理与控制决策耦合，规定步骤可能被遗漏或错误执行。

Method: 将技能知识融入状态内的局部指令以引导推理与生成；由状态机记录执行进度与中间结果，并用显式转移条件决定后续操作。增量编译器先把技能条款与工具接口映射为状态操作、局部指令、数据绑定和转移，再将开发轨迹与已有状态对齐以识别缺失操作与依赖，通过新增或复用状态并细化连接来整合；更新需通过静态检查并重放当前及此前所有已接受轨迹后才被接受。

Result: 在四个基准和四种执行器上，HEXIS 相比 Skill + ReAct 平均成功率提升 16.1 个百分点；在 Qwen3.8-27B 上，各基准执行 token 减少 38.4–88.9%。

Conclusion: 将技能编译为扩展有限状态机、分离知识与控制流，可显著提升智能体任务成功率并大幅降低执行开销。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable knowledge and instructions, yet agents must repeatedly infer how to apply them and which operation should follow. This couples task reasoning with control decisions, allowing prescribed steps to be omitted or applied incorrectly. We introduce HEXIS, which compiles agent skills into extended finite state machines that separate knowledge from control flow. Skill knowledge is incorporated into local instructions that guide reasoning and generation within states. The machine records execution progress and intermediate results, while explicit transition conditions determine subsequent operations. Our incremental compiler first maps skill clauses and tool interfaces to state operations, local instructions, data bindings, and transitions. It then aligns development traces with existing states to identify missing operations and dependencies. These are incorporated by adding or reusing states and refining their connections. Updates are accepted only after static checks and replay of the current and all previously accepted traces. Across four benchmarks and four executors, HEXIS improves success over Skill + ReAct by 16.1 percentage points on average. Qwen3.8-27B reduces execution tokens by 38.4-88.9% across benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于假设驱动的仿真工作流，用于在部署前筛选客户体验（CX）智能体；在Nubank的Card Delivery与Card Management智能体上验证，仿真与生产评估高度相关，并指导实现了tNPS提升36.69分和自助服务率提升8.82个百分点的线上效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CX智能体需在受监管场景下准确识别意图、遵循复杂运营政策并可靠调用工具，但人工端到端测试覆盖有限，线上实验又会让客户暴露于失败风险、损害信任。因此需要一种在部署前可大规模、低风险筛选候选智能体的方法。

Method: 提出假设驱动的仿真工作流：使用合成客户对智能体回复做出反应，并用模拟工具输出支撑多步智能体流程，从而无需调用生产后端。在Nubank的Card Delivery智能体及其后继Card Management上使用Snowglobe模拟器，并在16000余次仿真对话中筛选开放权重模型配置。

Result: 在4个已部署版本上，仿真与生产的版本级二元评估器得分高度相关；仿真引导的迭代使线上A/B测试中tNPS提升36.69分；后续A/B测试中，选出的模型将自助服务率提升8.82个百分点至Nubank历史最高水平，且tNPS无统计显著变化。

Conclusion: 仿真使模型、推理设置与提示词的大范围探索成为可能，且无需让客户暴露于风险，从而实现了仅靠线上实验难以推进的生产改进。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [103] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge 是一个不直接给出问题、只提供一堆旧文档（信件、收据、日志边角）的基准，模型需自行挖掘其中唯一的逻辑谜题并求解；谜题由生成器生成并用 SAT 求解器证明唯一性与每条线索的必要性。头号指标为“直觉”（仅凭故事成功答题），次要指标为世界重建。25 个前沿模型在 600 多实例（17,400 条评分记录）、三种匹配条件下评测，直觉指标使排行榜重排：差距达 22 倍，而事实恢复仅差 1.6 倍；第二名事实恢复者仅排第十四，有的模型对是否被告知问题无感，有的则在无问题条件下显著更好。部分模型在接触谜题前即被自身内容过滤器拦截，说明把拒答计为失败的基准其实在测量过滤器行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多直接把问题递给模型，考察的是给定问题后的推理与检索，而非模型能否在无明确提问的情况下主动发现问题。作者希望测量一种更接近真实情境的能力——“直觉”：面对杂乱原始文档，模型能否自行重建世界、识别出隐含的唯一逻辑谜题。同时，由于实例靠生成而非收集，语料可永久更新，避免数据污染；并通过 SAT 求解器证书保证题目解唯一、每条线索不可或缺。

Method: 基准构造上，EnigmaForge 向模型提供旧文档堆（letters、receipts、logbook margins），不提供问题；谜题被埋藏在文本中，解唯一性由生成时的 SAT 求解器证明，并附 ablation certificate 表明每条线索都是 load-bearing。评测设置：25 个前沿模型，600+ 实例，共 17,400 条评分记录，在三种匹配条件下对比。核心指标为 task success（只给故事时的成功率，即“直觉”），次要指标为 world reconstruction。

Result: 直觉指标显著改变排行榜：整体成功率差距达 22 倍，而单纯事实恢复能力的差距仅 1.6 倍；事实恢复第二名的模型在直觉排名中仅列第十四。个别模型对是否被告知问题表现无差异，另一个模型在无问题条件下反而显著更优。此外，若干模型在到达谜题前即被自身内容过滤器阻止，说明将拒答计为失败的基准实则在测量过滤器行为。

Conclusion: 论文主张：以“是否被给出问题”为核心差异的评测能揭示现有基准掩盖的能力差异，直觉（主动发现问题）与事实恢复是彼此分离的能力轴；生成式可更新语料与可验证唯一解、线索必备性的设计提升了评测可靠性；同时提醒社区，拒答类结果会污染基准分数，需与真实失败区分对待。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [104] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向搜索查询理解（QU）的搜索引擎感知强化学习框架，采用"先蒸馏后强化学习"范式：先用教师-学生监督微调获得符合schema的策略初始化，再针对每个QU组件设计基于搜索引擎实时交互的专属奖励进行优化，在Roblox搜索上使NDCG@20较SFT策略提升8.9点、较单一端到端奖励训练提升3.5点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产级搜索系统中，查询理解（QU）需要将原始用户查询转化为搜索执行计划以驱动检索与排序。大语言模型虽可将QU建模为结构化多任务生成问题（如意图分类、查询扩展），但如何优化这类模型以产出与搜索引擎耦合的输出仍是难题：静态的、基于标签的监督无法刻画各组件与底层搜索流水线交互后对下游性能的实际影响。

Method: 提出基于"先蒸馏后强化学习"（distill-then-RL）的搜索感知强化学习框架。第一阶段用教师-学生监督微调（SFT）得到结构良好、符合schema的策略初始化；第二阶段用强化学习优化，其奖励并非单一地绑定最终搜索结果，而是来自与在线搜索引擎的实时交互，并针对每个QU组件的具体操作角色定制专属奖励。

Result: 在Roblox搜索上的实验表明，这种组件级优化同时提升了各组件自身的效用与下游搜索质量：NDCG@20相较SFT策略提升8.9点，相较使用单一端到端奖励训练提升3.5点。

Conclusion: 为查询理解设计组件级、基于真实搜索管道交互的强化学习奖励，比静态标签监督或单一端到端奖励更能有效优化搜索系统的下游表现，验证了搜索感知RL框架在工业搜索场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [105] [Jev-Mobile: Jev as an Executor for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30186)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-Mobile 提出低频 VLM 规划与高频轻量执行的解耦范式，在 AndroidWorld 上以 79% 成功率显著降低时延（32.7%）与 API 成本（73.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主移动 GUI 智能体通常在几乎每一步交互中都依赖 VLM 同时完成规划与动作落地（action grounding），导致显著的推理延迟与模型服务成本，效率低且难以规模化部署。

Method: 将高层推理与底层执行解耦：VLM 以低频方式指定局部目标；可访问性树（accessibility tree）定义结构化可执行动作空间；由快速类型化决策模型 Jev 在该空间内高频反复选择动作，从而在单次 VLM 决策下执行多个 GUI 动作，在保留自适应交互能力的同时减少昂贵的 VLM 推理。

Result: 在完整 AndroidWorld 任务套件上，Jev-Mobile 任务成功率为 79%，SeeAct-V 为 78%，逐步（Step-wise）VLM 基线为 84%。在成功轨迹中，相较逐步 VLM 基线，端到端平均执行时间减少 32.7%，平均模型 API 成本降低 73.4%。

Conclusion: 将高层 VLM 推理与底层动作执行相解耦，可在保持有竞争力任务性能的同时大幅提升移动 GUI 智能体的效率，为低成本、低延迟的移动端智能体设计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for autonomous mobile GUI agents, but most existing systems rely on the VLM for both planning and action grounding at nearly every interaction step, leading to substantial latency and model-serving cost. We introduce Jev-Mobile, which shifts this paradigm to low-frequency VLM planning and high-frequency lightweight execution: the VLM specifies local goals, the accessibility tree defines a structured executable action space, and Jev, a fast typed decision model, repeatedly selects actions within this space. This design allows multiple GUI actions to be executed under a single VLM decision, reducing expensive VLM inference while preserving adaptive interaction. On the full AndroidWorld task suite, Jev-Mobile achieves 79% task success, compared with 78% for SeeAct-V and 84% for a Step-wise VLM baseline. Among successful trajectories, it reduces mean end-to-end execution time by 32.7% and mean model API cost by 73.4% relative to Step-wise VLM. These results show that decoupling high-level VLM reasoning from low-level action execution can substantially improve mobile GUI agent efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [106] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型在稀疏奖励下的长程推理脆弱性问题，提出理论框架SCA与统一方法SAGE，通过结构引导缓解探索偏差与累积偏差，在12个基准和7个模型家族上取得优于基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理在稀疏奖励下仍是LLM的核心挑战。作者认为其脆弱性源于复杂推理空间带来的两种偏差：探索偏差（模型倾向于局部看似合理但结构不稳定的分支）与累积偏差（局部小偏差随深度累积并抑制稀有奖励）。

Method: 首先提出符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论视角，刻画分支结构与稀疏奖励如何在局部可容许的长程推理中诱发上述偏差，并作为设计结构先验的原则。基于此提出SAGE（结构可容许引导探索）统一框架，包含两种互补的结构引导：代数稀疏化（将局部可容许候选投影到算子索引的代数子空间，抑制虚假分支以缓解探索偏差）与双曲结构引导（将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间，提供稠密的深度信号以缓解累积偏差）。

Result: 在12个基准和7个模型家族上，SAGE优于竞争性基线；特别是在Andrews-Curtis问题这一开放的真实长程任务上，最高实现了8倍提升。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 结构引导（代数稀疏化与双曲嵌入）能有效缓解长程推理中的探索偏差与累积偏差，为LLM在稀疏奖励下的长程推理提供了理论与方法上的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [107] [ExplorationBench: Measuring AI Systems' Exploration in Verifiable Alien Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30199)
*Ming Zhang,Zhenghao Xiang,Peizhong Gao,Yujiong Shen,Yuhui Wang,Zhonghan Yue,Shihan Dou,Zhangyue Yin,Junjie Ye,Shichun Liu,Weihuang Zheng,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Hongzhang Liu,Jiaqi Shao,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ExplorationBench，通过可验证的"异世界"沙盒（AlienCode、AlienLogic）评估 AI 系统的科学探索能力，避免答案无法核验与依赖预训练记忆的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现始于已知问题终结之处，AI 需具备探索能力：提出假设、设计实验并迭代。但评估困难有二：无法验证"全新"假设是否成立；无法区分系统是真正探索发现，还是从预训练数据中"回忆"出相关答案。

Method: 构建 ExplorationBench：以"异世界"为载体的可验证框架。异世界规则可执行，答案能被精确校验；其规则与常见知识冲突，故仅靠记忆无法解题。含两个沙盒：AlienCode（31 个发现目标，70 个任务）与 AlienLogic（24 个发现目标，70 个任务）。每个沙盒提供有缺陷的手册、任务相关的环境反馈与专用工具调用模式，系统据此探索后再完成留出任务。共评估 10 个 AI 系统。

Result: 最强系统能够习得并应用陌生规则；但性能在不同轨迹间差异显著，持续探索可能停滞甚至逆转此前的收益。

Conclusion: ExplorationBench 是迈向"能在未知环境中通过探索获取并应用全新知识"的 AI 系统的一步。

Abstract: Scientific discovery begins where known problems end. There, AI systems must engage in exploration: framing hypotheses, designing experiments, and iterating on the results. However, evaluating this ability is difficult: (1) how to verify whether a genuinely new hypothesis holds, and (2) how to determine whether a system has discovered it through exploration or merely recalled related knowledge from pre-training data. To this end, we introduce ExplorationBench, which turns the wicked problem of evaluating scientific exploration into a concrete and tractable framework built on verifiable Alien Worlds: their rules are executable, so every answer can be checked exactly, and they conflict with familiar knowledge, so recall alone cannot solve the tasks. The benchmark contains two sandboxes, AlienCode (31 discovery targets, 70 tasks) and AlienLogic (24 discovery targets, 70 tasks). Each sandbox provides a flawed manual, task-specific environmental feedback, and a dedicated tool-call schema. Systems use these resources to explore the sandbox, then solve held-out tasks. We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [108] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可扩展框架，从纵向电子健康记录(EHR)笔记自动生成问答对，构建可持续更新的临床LLM评测基准BRIE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的临床助手正被集成进EHR系统，其安全性与实用性依赖严格评测。但现有基准多为人工构建、成本高昂、更新困难，且随技术快速演进迅速过时，因此需要可扩展、可持续维护的评测方案。

Method: 设计从纵向EHR笔记自动生成问答对的框架，并由19名临床医生对生成器进行验证；据此构建BRIE基准，并在9个LLM与5种推理策略上进行评测。由于生成器经验证，BRIE可生成多个反映临床推理差异的答案，并可持续刷新内容以防止数据泄漏。

Result: 在最先进系统上，模型常遗漏临床关键信息，尤其在需要跨多份文档与多次就诊进行综合的问题上表现更差。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基准生成方法能够对临床LLM进行严格且及时更新的评测，支持在快速演变的医疗场景中部署前的有效评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [109] [AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30264)
*Jiabin Qiu,Zixuan Chen,Hongye Cao,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AD-WM动作判别式世界模型，通过保留动作信息提升反事实MPC规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常只训练预测真实转移，但MPC需要从同一状态比较不同动作。仅优化事实预测误差的模型可能无法区分候选动作，导致规划失效。

Method: AD-WM结合残差潜动力学与预测器级动作恢复正则化，采用逆动力学和基于条件互信息的归一化恢复目标，促使规划转移保留动作信息；辅助头在测试时丢弃，MPC流程不变。

Result: OGBench-Cube困难起始成功率从3.7%提升至52.0%；五个仿真环境中四个优于基线。规划诊断显示事实预测误差与整体动作排序不匹配闭环成功率，而CEM对齐的精英遗憾更贴合成功。冻结V-JEPA 2编码器与匹配DROID后训练下，零样本迁移Franka抓放成功率由42.2%升至71.1%。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型应保留反事实选择所需的动作相关差异，而非仅优化事实预测精度。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically trained to predict factual transitions, whereas model predictive control (MPC) must compare alternative actions from the same state. A model can therefore achieve low factual prediction error yet poorly distinguish candidate actions. We introduce AD-WM, an action-discriminative joint-embedding world model for counterfactual MPC. AD-WM combines residual latent dynamics with predictor-level action-recovery regularization, using inverse dynamics and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information. Both objectives encourage planning transitions to preserve action information; their auxiliary heads are discarded at test time, leaving MPC unchanged. On OGBench-Cube, AD-WM improves hard-start success from 3.7% to 52.0% over a matched LeWM baseline and improves mean success over the reproduced baseline in four of five simulation environments. Planning diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not follow the closed-loop success ordering, whereas CEM-aligned elite regret tracks success more closely. With a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder and matched DROID post-training, AD-WM also improves zero-shot transfer to our Franka setup, increasing basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation. These results suggest that world models for planning should preserve action-dependent differences needed for counterfactual selection, rather than optimize factual prediction accuracy alone. More videos and code are available at https://ad-wm.github.io/.

</details>


### [110] [Forecast-Dojo: Replayable Environments for Benchmarking and Training LLM Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28876)
*Liqin Ye,Haorui Wang,Fardin Ahmed,Rongzhi Zhang,Yuan He,Ziyuan Lin,Yanbin Yin,Jing Peng,Michael Galarnyk,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Forecast-Dojo 可重放环境，用预测市场问题与带日期新闻评测和训练 LLM 预测智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 预测智能体缺乏可重复评测与可训练的一体化环境，且新事件需等待结果才能反馈，成本高、不可重放。

Method: 整合已解决预测市场事件与带日期新闻；含 1568 个 Polymarket 事件（按时间划分训练/评测期）与 1880 万篇带日期新闻；支持同一任务与工具下重复评测、交互轨迹采集与结果反馈；评测 12 个模型并测试研究工具、信念笔记本与监督微调。

Result: 研究工具使全部 12 个模型的 Brier 分数下降；随事件推进预测改善，新增证据更多时提升最大；但所有模型在 Brier 分数与准确率上仍落后于历史市场预测；信念笔记本降低研究成本但未稳定提升预测质量。

Conclusion: Forecast-Dojo 可作为 LLM 预测智能体的评测与训练平台，并提供交互轨迹与结果反馈，监督微调验证了其训练可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-Dojo, a replayable environment for benchmarking and training LLM forecasting agents. It combines resolved prediction-market questions with dated news, allowing agents to research an event and revisit their predictions at successive historical dates. The same tasks and tools support repeated evaluation, collection of training interactions, and feedback from recorded outcomes without waiting for new events to resolve. Forecast-Dojo contains 1,568 Polymarket events, split by time into training and evaluation periods, and 18.8M dated news articles. In an evaluation of 12 models, research tools lower Brier score for all 12. Forecasts also improve as events unfold, with the largest gains at steps where more newly dated evidence is recorded. Every model still trails historical market forecasts in both Brier score and accuracy. A belief notebook carried between dates lowers research cost but does not consistently improve forecast quality. Beyond evaluation, Forecast-Dojo provides interaction trajectories and outcome feedback for agent learning, with supervised fine-tuning as a proof of concept.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28682)
*Zehao Liu,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"思维泄漏"（thinking leakage）概念并给出因果中介分析框架，发现NoThink模式下后训练带来的性能提升很大程度源于模型向Think行为的漂移，而非NoThink能力的真正提升，泄漏比例达42%–79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练混合推理模型在NoThink模式下能提升性能且保持推理速度，但作者质疑这些收益可能并非来自NoThink能力的真正增强，而是借用了基座模型Think模式中已有的思维能力，因此需要因果性地审计这一"思维泄漏"的贡献。

Method: 将思维泄漏形式化为因果中介框架，沿一条由基座模型导出的简单激活方向进行双向干预：行为与表征分析、沿该方向对基座模型进行引导（steering）、对后训练检查点进行反向引导（counter-steering）。实验覆盖三个模型、三种后训练方法以及竞赛数学基准。

Result: 泄漏真实、具因果性且显著：行为与表征分析显示模型向Think漂移；沿该方向引导基座模型可复现后训练带来的大部分准确率增益；对检查点反向引导可消除其相当比例的增益。在九个NoThink增益为正的对齐检查点上，泄漏比例介于42%至79%。

Conclusion: 后训练方法的表面优势可能反映的是向Think行为更大的漂移，而非在NoThink模式下提升能力，这使得方法之间的比较可能被混淆；研究提示需要区分真正的能力提升与对既有Think行为的重新调用。

Abstract: Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. However, these gains may draw on thinking behavior already accessible through the base model's Think mode. We formulate this thinking leakage in a causal mediation framework and audit its contribution using bidirectional interventions along a simple base-derived activation direction. Across three models and three post-training methods on competition math benchmarks, we find that leakage is real, causal, and substantial: behavioral and representational analyses reveal shifts toward Think, steering the base model along this direction reproduces most of the post-training accuracy gain, and counter-steering a checkpoint removes a substantial share of what it gains. Across nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the resulting leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. These interventions support a substantial causal contribution of thinking leakage. Our findings show that a post-training method's apparent advantage can therefore reflect greater drift toward Think, obscuring whether it improves capability within NoThink or more effectively re-invokes existing Think behavior.

</details>


### [112] [CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Jiapu Wang,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CataOPD将教师模型作为"催化剂"而非目标，通过自救援路由、催化引导自解决和障碍加权内化三阶段机制，扩展可达性并将已验证的学生轨迹内化为无催化剂策略，在推理任务上超越现有基线并提升分布外泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）与在线策略蒸馏（OPD）是提升大语言模型推理能力的两种代表性范式，但各有局限：当采样不到正确轨迹时，RL缺乏正向正确性信号；而OPD受限于学生在在线策略分布下可达的推理轨迹。因此需要一种新方法来扩展可达性，同时避免推理时依赖教师。

Method: 提出CataOPD，让教师充当催化剂而非目标。包含三个组件：(1) Self-Rescue Routing——将经验上全部失败的组作为路由信号（而非教师干预触发条件），先通过额外的在线策略自采样寻找正确轨迹；(2) Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution——对自救援后仍未解决的问题，利用催化引导在引导后的学生分布中引出已验证的学生生成轨迹；(3) Barrier-Weighted Internalization——按引导到无引导的对数概率差距对token加权，将更新集中在无引导时难以决策的关键token上，最终内化为无催化剂策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，CataOPD优于当前基线，将学生的独立推理能力扩展到仍未恢复的问题上，并在无催化剂推理条件下提升了分布外泛化能力。项目已开源。

Conclusion: CataOPD通过将教师重新定位为催化剂，有效结合RL与OPD的优势，实现无催化剂推理时的性能提升与泛化改善。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD) are two representative paradigms for improving large language model reasoning. However, when no correct trajectory is sampled, RL lacks a positive correctness signal, while OPD remains constrained by the reasoning trajectories reachable under the student's on-policy distribution. Therefore, we propose CataOPD, where the teacher acts as a catalyst rather than a target, expanding reachability while internalizing verified student-produced trajectories into a catalyst-free policy. Self-Rescue Routing uses empirically all-failed groups as routing signals rather than teacher-intervention triggers, first seeking correct trajectories through additional on-policy self-sampling. For problems unresolved after self-rescue, Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution uses catalytic guidance to elicit a verified student-produced trajectory in the guided student distribution. Barrier-Weighted Internalization weights tokens by guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, focusing updates on decisive tokens difficult without guidance. Experimental results show that CataOPD outperforms current baselines, extends independent student reasoning to still-unrecovered problems, and improves out-of-distribution generalization under catalyst-free inference. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD.

</details>


### [113] [Graph, Loop, and Harness Engineering for Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering and Analytical Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29668)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两个零信任智能体框架（数据工程与OLAP分析），以证据门控实现可信云数据工程与分析执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体虽在自动化数据工作流上进展迅速，但端到端云数据工程与分析执行需在代码、数据、基础设施与运行时环境间可靠协同，缺乏可信验证机制。

Method: 提出零信任智能体数据工程（从自然语言任务生成、部署并验证完整云数据工程方案，完成条件依赖仓库、部署、运行时与策略证据）与零信任智能体OLAP（受治理的数据准备结合可验证在线分析处理，仅在验证与证据绑定审批后推进生产，答案仅在快照一致执行、结果精确等价、确定性溯源与反思后发布）；共享图工程、循环工程与智能体挂架工程三种抽象。

Result: 在正常执行、受控故障、有界恢复与策略约束条件下评估两框架，度量验证完成、恢复、授权执行、生产晋升与可验证OLAP执行等指标。

Conclusion: 基于证据门控与零信任执行的框架可提升云数据工程与分析的可靠性与可信度，为智能体自动化数据工作流提供可验证保障。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly automate data workflows, but end-to-end cloud data engineering and analytical execution require reliable coordination across code, data, infrastructure, and runtime environments. We present two zero-trust frameworks. Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering generates, deploys, and verifies complete cloud data-engineering solutions from natural-language tasks, with completion conditioned on repository, deployment, runtime, and policy evidence. Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP combines governed Data Preparation with verified Online Analytical Processing (OLAP), permitting production promotion only after validation and evidence-bound approval, and releasing analytical answers only after Same-Snapshot Execution, Exact Result Equivalence, deterministic grounding, and reflection. Both frameworks share three abstractions: graph engineering for evidence-gated workflow structure, loop engineering for bounded recovery, and agent-harness engineering for zero-trust execution. We evaluate both frameworks under nominal execution, controlled failures, bounded recovery, and policy-constrained conditions, measuring verified completion, recovery, authorization enforcement, production promotion, and verified OLAP execution.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Average Safety: Chance-Constrained LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29960)
*Taha Entesari,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机会约束的安全保持微调框架，通过可控违约率约束限制微调后安全性能退化，并给出闭式约束感知梯度更新，实验优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新目标上微调大语言模型可提升有用性、指令遵循或领域性能，但会导致安全关键提示上的性能退化。现有安全保持方法多以平均安全损失或加权辅助惩罚为约束，容易掩盖罕见但严重的失败案例，因此需要从可靠性约束角度而非平均风险正则化来处理安全问题。

Method: 作者提出机会约束形式的安全保持微调，限制相对于参考模型退化超过给定阈值的安全样本比例。由于经验机会约束包含不连续指示函数，引入违约率的可微majorization，得到可处理的保守约束；进而设计约束感知梯度下降方法，将majorized约束视为参数空间中的安全集，对微调方向做最小修改以保持可行性，更新具有闭式解，形成关注阈值附近及以上的尾部感知安全校正。

Result: 在三种不同任务、三个模型上进行了大量有害微调实验，结果表明该方法一致优于现有文献中的基线。

Conclusion: LLM微调中的安全保持更适合被视为可靠性约束优化问题，而不是平均风险正则化问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new objectives can improve helpfulness, instruction following, or domain-specific performance, but it can also induce regressions on safety-critical prompts. Existing safety-preserving fine-tuning methods typically control average safety loss or use weighted auxiliary penalties, which can obscure rare but severe failures. We propose a chance-constrained formulation for safety-preserving fine-tuning that limits the fraction of safety examples whose degradation relative to a reference model exceeds a prescribed threshold. Because the resulting empirical chance constraint contains a discontinuous indicator, we introduce a differentiable majorization of the violation rate, yielding a tractable conservative constraint. We then develop a constraint-aware gradient descent method that treats the majorized constraint as a safe set in parameter space and minimally modifies the fine-tuning direction to preserve feasibility. The resulting update admits a closed form and produces a tail-aware safety correction that emphasizes examples near or above the degradation threshold. We conduct an extensive set of experiments on harmful fine-tuning across three different tasks and three models and show that our approach consistently outperforms the baselines that exist in the literature. These results suggest that safety preservation in LLM fine-tuning is better viewed as a reliability-constrained optimization problem than as average-risk regularization.

</details>


### [115] [Let Training Guide Selection: Online Synthetic Data Filtering via Real-Anchored Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29988)
*Yanran Wu,Sana Lakdawala,Renzo Tassara Miller,Chongyang Bai,Sharath Ciddu,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Kungang Li,Sandeep Pandey,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FROST，一个基于梯度反馈的在线合成数据筛选框架，用真实训练数据锚定样本效用，自适应决定何时筛选与保留哪些样本，无需外部验证器或留出验证集，在图像分类、LLM 文本转SQL微调及工业广告重排序中均取得增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据可在真实数据有限时扩展监督信号，但噪声与分布不匹配会削弱其价值。现有合成数据选择方法多关注保真度或多样性，未考虑学习者在训练过程中不断变化的需求，因此需要一个能动态评估合成数据效用的在线筛选机制。

Method: FROST 通过以真实训练数据为锚点的梯度反馈估计合成样本的效用；对批次效用与近期历史进行校准，判断当前批次是否处于"带外"（out-of-band）从而决定是否需要过滤，仅在带外批次中执行样本筛选以决定保留哪些样本；整个过程在线进行，不依赖外部验证器或留出的验证集。

Result: 在图像分类与面向文本转SQL的LLM微调两个公开基准上，FROST 过滤掉约20%–30%的合成数据，同时相比在全量合成数据池上训练提升了真实任务性能；进一步应用于大规模工业广告重排序系统，在高度优化的生产基线之上取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: FROST 表明基于梯度反馈的在线合成数据筛选能够在保证真实任务性能的前提下有效去除冗余或有害合成样本，方法无需额外验证资源，具有良好的有效性与泛化能力，适用于视觉、语言及工业级推荐场景。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale training supervision when real-world data are limited, but noise and distribution mismatch can reduce its value. Existing synthetic data selection methods often emphasize fidelity or diversity rather than the learner's evolving needs. We propose FROST, an online framework that estimates synthetic-data utility through gradient feedback anchored in real training data. It calibrates batch utility against recent history to determine when filtering is needed and filters samples only in out-of-band batches to determine what to retain, without an external verifier or held-out validation set. Experiments on two public benchmarks for image classification and LLM fine-tuning for text-to-SQL show that FROST filters out around 20--30% of the synthetic data while improving real-task performance compared with training on the full synthetic data pool. We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [116] [Residual Correlation as a Diagnostic for Joint-Uncertainty Gains from GP Coregionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30085)
*Fangqin Zhou,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多目标回归中多输出高斯过程（GP-ICM）的共区域化耦合何时真正有用，提出轻量诊断量 D_logdet 预测联合不确定性量化收益，并给出 Residual-ICM 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标回归常假设相关目标通过共区域化共享统计强度能提升性能，但实际收益不稳定。作者希望厘清共区域化的真正价值来源，并找出能预测其何时有效的指标。

Method: 作者提出诊断量 D_logdet = -1/2 log det R_res（可由独立GP计算），用于衡量建模完整残差协方差相对于对角协方差的理想化联合NLL收益；并据此提出 Residual-ICM，在保留独立边际方差的同时向联合协方差加入残差相关结构。验证涵盖受控合成实验、16个多目标基准，以及冻结的Transformer和CNN表示用于关键点回归。

Result: 点预测几乎不变（ΔR²≈0）；共区域化的主要收益在联合不确定性量化而非点预测。原始目标相关性无法预测耦合是否有用，残差相关性才是最强预测因子。D_logdet 强预测观察到的ICM NLL改善（ρ_s=-0.83，p<0.001），优于特征样本比等启发式。Residual-ICM 在所比较方法中取得最佳平均联合NLL。

Conclusion: 共区域化的价值主要在于联合不确定性量化；D_logdet 是一个有效且轻量的诊断工具，可判断协方差耦合何时有用。该诊断仅适用于可分离共区域化所捕捉的全局高斯残差依赖结构。

Abstract: In multi-target regression, correlated targets are often coupled through multi-output Gaussian processes with an intrinsic model of coregionalisation (GP-ICM), assuming that sharing statistical strength improves overall performance. In practice, the benefits are inconsistent. Across the settings studied, we find that the main benefit of coregionalisation is joint uncertainty quantification rather than point prediction. Raw target correlation does not predict when coupling helps; in the separable GP-ICM settings studied here, residual correlation, the cross-target dependence left unexplained by independent per-target predictors, is the strongest predictor of joint-uncertainty gains.
  We introduce a lightweight diagnostic, $D_{\rm logdet}=-\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$, which represents the idealised joint negative log-likelihood (NLL) gain from modelling a full rather than diagonal residual covariance and is computable from independent GPs alone. Across a controlled synthetic study, 16 multi-target benchmarks, and frozen transformer and convolutional neural network representations for keypoint regression, point prediction remains largely unchanged ($ΔR^2\approx 0$). In contrast, $D_{\rm logdet}$ strongly predicts observed ICM NLL improvements ($ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$), outperforming heuristics such as the feature-to-sample ratio. We also propose Residual-ICM, which preserves independent marginal variances while adding residual-correlation structure to the joint covariance. Residual-ICM achieves the best average joint NLL among the compared methods, while the diagnostic indicates when covariance coupling is likely to be useful. The diagnostic is specific to global Gaussian residual dependence, the structure captured by separable coregionalisation.

</details>


### [117] [AT-SKM-Net: An Accelerated Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Framework for Linear Hard-Constraint Feasibility on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30088)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yao Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AT-SKM Net框架，用拓扑感知异质GNN引导混合采样并引入Cholesky更新，将等式投影复杂度从O(N^3)降至O(N^2)，在保持零约束违反的同时大幅减少迭代次数并加速SKM层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构线性约束优化是关键基础设施的基础，但面临海量严格硬约束与高维带来的可扩展性瓶颈。已有投影类方法（如T-SKM-Net）虽能保证可行性，却需处理全部约束集并进行昂贵矩阵分解，动态环境下计算成本过高。

Method: 提出Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net框架：一是引入由拓扑感知异质GNN模型引导的混合采样策略，将计算集中在活跃约束上并消除冗余计算；二是采用Cholesky Update机制处理图约束的拓扑变化，在低秩扰动下将等式投影复杂度由O(N^3)降至O(N^2)。

Result: 在随机几何图、N-1安全约束DC-OPF以及最小成本天然气输运问题上验证：AT-SKM将迭代次数最多减少85%，实现2.95倍至7.29倍的SKM层加速，同时保持零约束违反。

Conclusion: AT-SKM通过GNN引导采样与Cholesky更新，显著提升图结构约束优化的求解效率与可扩展性，同时不牺牲可行性保证。

Abstract: Graph-structured optimization with linear constraints is fundamental to critical infrastructure but faces scalability limits due to massive strict hard constraints and high dimensionality. While recent projection-based methods such as Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Net (T-SKM-Net) guarantee feasibility, they face high computational costs in dynamic environments by processing the entire constraint set and requiring expensive matrix factorizations. To bridge this gap, we propose the Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net framework. To concentrate computation on the active constraints and eliminate redundant calculations, we introduce a hybrid sampling strategy guided by a topology-aware heterogeneous GNN model. To efficiently handle topological shifts in graph-based constraints, we employ a Cholesky Update mechanism that theoretically reduces the equality projection complexity from O(N^3) to O(N^2) under low-rank perturbations. Experiments on random geometric graphs, N-1 Security-Constrained DC-OPF, and minimum-cost gas transport problem demonstrate that AT-SKM reduces iteration counts by up to 85% and achieves 2.95x-7.29x SKM layer speedups, while maintaining zero constraint violations.

</details>


### [118] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对任意各向同性对数凹分布，一类多项式是平方和，去除了Kothari-Steinhardt定理中对Poincaré常数的依赖，恢复最优矩界并给出高维统计估计的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kothari与Steinhardt的定理在对数凹分布上给出平方和证书，但其界依赖Poincaré常数，无法达到最优矩界。本文旨在消除这一依赖，恢复对数凹分布的最优矩界，并由此获得维度无关误差的高维统计估计算法。

Method: 利用随机局部化（stochastic localization）将分布P分解为随机强对数凹测度的平均，这些测度的中心矩满足Diakonikolas、Hopkins、Pensia与Tiegel的子高斯证书。通过协方差自适应的局部化选择，证明由Letwin关于二次型方差不等式导出的四阶矩证书足以在每个偶数阶控制该平均。

Result: 证明了对任意各向同性对数凹分布P和任意偶数m≥2，多项式(Cm)^m‖v‖₂^m − E⟨X,v⟩^m是平方和（C为通用常数）。作为直接推论，获得了一系列高维统计估计问题的高效算法，且误差保证与维度无关。

Conclusion: 该工作去除了现有平方和定理对Poincaré常数的依赖，恢复了对数凹分布的最优矩界，并为高维统计估计提供了维度无关误差的高效算法，方法上结合随机局部化与四阶矩证书。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对潜空间神经代理求解器在长时自回归推演中误差快速累积的问题，指出不稳定性源于仅为重建而训练的潜表示，并通过一系列训练层面的干预手段，使长推演误差降低约40%，在保持高精度的同时大幅节省算力与显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间动力学模型通过演化压缩后的潜空间来加速含时物理系统模拟，理论上可降低计算成本并简化学习。但实际中，长时自回归推演会使误差迅速累积，严重限制其预测可用性。作者希望厘清该不稳定的根源，并寻找有效的训练层面解决方案。

Method: 作者首先论证不稳定性并非来自潜表示本身，而是源于仅以重建为目标的训练方式，使表示不适合长时程预测。为此系统评估了若干训练层面的干预：用Koopman算子学习对齐潜表示与长时程推演；在自编码器训练中注入Hamming噪声以改进压缩；以及通过噪声注入与多步推演微调来改进动力学建模。

Result: 这些干预合起来使长推演误差降低约40%，在两个物理基准上达到或超过全分辨率模型的精度，同时所需浮点运算量少两个数量级、GPU显存仅需一半。应用于高周疲劳的介观晶体塑性模拟时，所得代理模型能在远超训练所见的时程上实现稳定外推。研究还发现，那些提升长推演稳定性的干预往往会降低重建与单步预测等常规指标。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅以降维为目标，而应重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化，这是科学应用中可靠且高效神经代理模型的关键要求。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>


### [120] [Minimally Invasive Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30218)
*Taha Entesari,Jingyu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MISVO方法，通过KL几何正则化实现预logit引导向量的最小侵入优化，在多数设置中取得最高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预logit引导通过向冻结语言模型的最终隐状态添加向量来适应测试时奖励，但无正则化的奖励优化会显著改变输出分布并降低生成质量，因此需要一种控制分布偏移的优化方法。

Method: MISVO利用诱导token分布的局部KL几何对干预进行惩罚，用Fisher二次型度量分布敏感性，并通过与冻结语言模型头的矩阵-向量乘积给出解析梯度；同时将序列级KL梯度精确分解为解析Fisher项和后缀score-function项，证明后缀项在固定生成视界下为引导幅度的二阶项，并采用冻结参考代理优化位置特定的干预，不更新模型参数。

Result: 在约1B–14B参数的模型上，于偏好和代码生成任务中，MISVO在七个模型-任务设置中的六个取得最高平均奖励，其多样性和连贯性得分接近Best-of-N。

Conclusion: MISVO通过局部KL几何正则化实现最小侵入的引导向量优化，在提升奖励的同时保持生成质量与多样性。

Abstract: Pre-logit steering adapts a frozen language model to a test-time reward by adding vectors to its final hidden states. Unregularized reward optimization can substantially alter the output distribution and degrade generation quality. We propose Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO), which penalizes interventions using the local KL geometry of the induced token distribution. The resulting Fisher quadratic measures distributional sensitivity and admits an analytic gradient computed through matrix--vector products with the frozen language-model head. We derive an exact decomposition of the sequence-level KL gradient into an analytic Fisher term and a suffix score-function term. For a fixed generation horizon, we show that the suffix term is second order in the steering magnitude and that three Fisher surrogates agree with the full KL gradient to first order. MISVO uses the frozen-reference surrogate to optimize position-specific interventions without updating model parameters. Across preference and code-generation tasks on models with approximately 1B--14B parameters, MISVO achieves the highest mean reward in six of seven model--task settings, with diversity and coherence scores close to those of Best-of-N.

</details>


### [121] [PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30226)
*Kimia Hamidieh,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PoEM 框架，无需重新运行强化学习，即可用已有奖励上后训练的模型预测新奖励函数的 RL 策略输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型常用 RL 后训练来最大化特定奖励，但该过程计算昂贵、有时不稳定，且每当奖励模型改变或需组合多个奖励时都必须从头运行。因此作者提出核心问题：给定新奖励函数，能否在不实际运行 RL 的情况下预测其 RL 结果？

Method: PoEM 利用一组已在其他奖励上后训练好的模型来预测新奖励下的 RL 输出。首先证明：若新奖励可写成已有奖励的线性组合，则新策略在对数空间上也可写成已有对数策略的线性组合。即使奖励之间非线性相关，RL 训练得到的对数策略往往跨越一个近似低秩子空间。组合权重系数仅需利用奖励模型或基策略在样本上的输出即可估计。基于此将其转化为一种算法，输入已后训练模型与新奖励函数，近似目标 RL 策略而不执行任何额外 RL 训练。

Result: 在合成奖励与真实奖励上进行了实验验证，覆盖文本与图像两种模态。

Conclusion: 该方法表明 RL 后训练结果在一定程度上可由已有后训练模型预测，从而避免重复且昂贵的 RL 训练，实现对新奖励函数的策略近似。

Abstract: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.

</details>
